Conclusion: 该工作展示了如何将基础模型的世界理解能力迁移至具身导航，使机器人能够受益于现有图像生成模型的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) images have been widely demonstrated to provide valuable prior information for navigation. Given the global information provided by such views, two key challenges remain: how to fully exploit this information and how to reliably use it during execution. In this paper, we propose a navigation system that uses BEV images as global priors and is designed for ground and near-ground robotic platforms. The system employs an image generation model to interpret human intent from natural language, identify the target destination, and generate traversability masks. During execution, we introduce cross-view localization to align the robot's odometry with the BEV map and mitigate long-term drift in conventional odometry. We conduct extensive benchmark experiments to evaluate the proposed method and further validate it on a UAV platform. Using only a conventional local motion planner, the UAV successfully completes a 160-meter outdoor long-range navigation task. This work demonstrates how the world-understanding capabilities of foundation models can be transferred to embodied navigation, enabling robots to benefit from the strong generalization ability of existing image generation models.

</details>


### [35] [Is the Future Compatible? Diagnosing Dynamic Consistency in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07514)
*Bo-Kai Ruan,Teng-Fang Hsiao,Ling Lo,Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出动作-状态一致性作为评估世界动作模型（WAM）可靠性的新维度，发现该指标能有效区分成功与失败的 rollout，并提出一种无需价值函数的共识策略用于测试时选择，在 RoboCasa 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型生成的未来预测缺乏对动作与状态转换之间一致性的评估，仅关注视觉合理性不足以判断决策可靠性。

Method: 系统研究联合预测模型和逆动力学模型，提出动作-状态一致性指标；发现背景坍缩问题；提出基于预测未来之间一致性的无价值函数共识策略。

Result: 一致性指标有效区分成功与失败的 rollout，趋势与价值函数估计一致；背景坍缩导致低动态失败轨迹具有欺骗性一致性；共识策略在 RoboCasa 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上提升成功率。

Conclusion: 动作-状态一致性既是评估 WAM 可靠性的诊断工具，也是无需价值函数的实用规划信号。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable decision-making through imagined rollouts by predicting future observations and actions. However, the reliability of these imagined futures remains under-examined: is a generated future merely visually plausible, or is it dynamically compatible with the action sequence it claims to model? In this work, we identify action-state consistency, the alignment between predicted actions and induced state transitions, as a missing reliability axis for WAMs. Through a systematic study across representative joint-prediction and inverse-dynamics models, we find that action-state consistency systematically separates successful and failed rollouts across many tasks and follows similar success-failure trends as learned value estimates. These results suggest that consistency captures decision-relevant structure beyond visual realism. We further identify background collapse as an important boundary condition, where low-dynamics failed trajectories can become deceptively consistent because static futures are easier to predict. Building on these findings, we introduce a value-free consensus strategy for test-time selection, which ranks candidate rollouts by agreement among predicted futures. This strategy improves success rates on RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 without additional training or reward modeling. Taken together, our findings establish action-state consistency as both a diagnostic tool for evaluating WAM reliability and a practical signal for value-free planning.

</details>


### [36] [Search-based Robustness Testing of Laptop Refurbishing Robotic Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07530)
*Erblin Isaku,Hassan Sartaj,Shaukat Ali,Malaika Din Hashmi,Francois Picard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE方法，一种基于多目标优化的搜索式鲁棒性测试方法，用于发现笔记本翻新机器人中目标检测模型在面对微小局部扰动时的失效情况。PROBE使用NSGA-II算法搜索最小扰动，实验表明其比随机搜索有效3-7倍，且发现的扰动可在不同模型间迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笔记本翻新机器人依赖目标检测模型识别螺丝、贴纸等物体，但模型对小输入扰动的鲁棒性不足可能导致翻新过程中损坏笔记本电脑。需要一种方法来系统性地检测这类模型的脆弱点。

Method: 提出PROBE方法，基于多目标优化（NSGA-II算法）进行搜索式鲁棒性测试。优化目标包括：诱导定位和置信度失效、最小化扰动幅度、发现多样化的失效案例。

Result: PROBE比随机搜索有效3-7倍（在生成失效诱导扰动方面），且所需扰动幅度更小；生成的扰动可在不同模型间迁移；变质关系能提供模型鲁棒性的额外洞察。

Conclusion: PROBE能有效发现目标检测模型在笔记本翻新机器人场景下的鲁棒性问题，为保障机器人安全操作、减少电子废弃物、支持循环经济提供了重要技术支撑。

Abstract: The Danish Technological Institute (DTI) focuses on transferring advanced technologies (including robots) to the industry and the public sector. One key application is laptop refurbishment using specialized robots, aimed at promoting reuse, reducing electronic waste, and supporting the European Circular Economy Action Plan. The software of such robots often includes features that use object detection models to detect objects for various purposes, such as identifying screws for laptop disassembly or detecting stickers to remove them. Ensuring the robustness of such models to small input variations remains a critical challenge, and addressing it is important to avoid potential damage to laptops during refurbishment. In this paper, we propose PROBE, a search-based robustness testing approach that leverages multi-objective optimization to identify minimal, localized perturbations that expose failures in object detection models used in the software of laptop refurbishing robots. PROBE employs NSGA-II to systematically explore the perturbation space, optimizing for failure induction considering both localization and confidence, and perturbation magnitude, while enabling the discovery of diverse failure cases. Results show that PROBE is 3$\times$ to 7$\times$ more effective than random search in generating failure-inducing perturbations, while requiring smaller perturbation magnitudes, and that the generated perturbations transfer across models. We further show that metamorphic relations provide additional insights into model robustness, enabling the assessment of stability even in non-failing cases.

</details>


### [37] [How to utilize failure demo data?: Effective data selection for imitation learning using distribution differences in attention mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07560)
*Kana Miyamoto,Kanata Suzuki,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用失败数据进行模仿学习的方法，通过学习成功-失败差异的潜在表征并将其融入注意力机制，在推理时从初始观察中选择合适的潜在模式以提高动作稳定性，并引入训练后指标选择有益失败样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习主要依赖成功示范，但数据收集中失败不可避免。现有利用失败数据的方法需要额外数据处理或自主 rollout 迭代更新，难以直接稳定地利用数据收集中积累的失败数据。

Method: 1) 学习成功-失败差异的潜在表征并融入注意力机制；2) 推理时从初始观测中选择合适的潜在模式以提高动作稳定性；3) 引入训练后量化指标，衡量每个失败样本与成功示范的注意力差异，用于筛选失败数据。

Result: 仿真结果表明所提方法在使用失败数据训练时提升了任务成功率，所提指标能识别出与成功示范结合时对学习有益的失败样本。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在机器人数据收集流程中更高效地利用收集到的示范数据。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotic tasks has relied primarily on policies trained only on successful demonstrations, although failures are unavoidable during human data collection. Many existing approaches for exploiting failure data require additional data processing or iterative policy updates through autonomous rollouts, making it difficult to directly and stably utilize failure data accumulated during data collection. In this work, we propose a method that learns latent representations of success-failure discrepancies and incorporates them into the attention mechanism. During inference, an appropriate latent mode is selected from the initial observation to improve action stability. Furthermore, we introduce a post-training metric that quantifies the attention discrepancy between each failure sample and successful demonstrations to select failure data. Simulation results show that the proposed method improves task success rates when trained with failure data and that the proposed metric identifies failure samples that are beneficial for learning when combined with successful demonstrations. These results suggest that the proposed method can support more efficient use of collected demonstrations in robotic data collection pipelines.

</details>


### [38] [BrickCraft: Visuomotor Skill Composition with Situated Manual Guidance for Long-Horizon Interlocking Brick Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07605)
*Jichuan Yu,Bowei Li,Zhenran Tang,Guanxing Lu,Chuxiong Hu,Ruixuan Liu,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了BrickCraft，一个用于长时序、可泛化的互锁积木自主装配的组合框架，通过相对公式化建模、情境化手册和组合执行流水线，实现了从少量演示中学习装配技能并泛化到未知结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人装配互锁积木需要同时解决长时序任务推理、空间定位和精细操作三大挑战，现有方法在这三方面的集成上存在不足。

Method: 1) 采用相对公式化建模，每步装配锚定于局部结构中的参考积木，将复杂任务分解为有限可复用的基本技能；2) 通过情境化手册（situated manuals），将装配意图投射到实时机器人观测上，为学习的视觉运动技能提供显式空间引导；3) 采用组合执行流水线，链式执行这些空间定位的技能以完成长时序装配任务。

Result: 实验表明BrickCraft能从有限演示中获取熟练的装配技能，并对未见过的结构展现出强大的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: BrickCraft有效弥合了高层装配规划与物理执行之间的鸿沟，为长时序、可泛化的自主积木装配提供了一种可行的组合式解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic assembly of interlocking bricks demands seamless integration of long-horizon task reasoning, spatial grounding, and fine-grained manipulation. This paper presents BrickCraft, a compositional framework designed for long-horizon and generalizable interlocking brick assembly. BrickCraft models the assembly process using a relative formulation, where each step is anchored to a reference brick within the partial structure, thereby decomposing complex tasks into a finite set of reusable primitive skills. BrickCraft bridges the gap between high-level assembly plans and physical execution through situated manuals, which provide explicit spatial guidance for learned visuomotor skills by projecting the assembly intent onto real-time robot observations. Finally, BrickCraft employs a compositional execution pipeline that chains these spatially grounded skills to accomplish long-horizon assembly tasks. Extensive experimental validations demonstrate that BrickCraft acquires proficient assembly skills from a limited set of demonstrations and exhibits strong compositional generalization to unseen structures. The project website is available at https://intelligent-control-lab.github.io/BrickCraft.

</details>


### [39] [PhySPRING: Structure-Preserving Reduction of Physics-Informed Twins via GNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07687)
*Yixiong Jing,Xingyuan Chen,Guangming Wang,Olaf Wysocki,Haibing Wu,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhySPRING是一种基于可微GNN的方法，用于简化弹簧-质点数字孪生系统，通过联合学习层级化粗化图拓扑及其机械参数，在保持物理和视觉保真度的同时实现高达2.30倍的加速，并在机器人策略评估中保持操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于物理的数字孪生方法通常从视觉重建继承分辨率，导致拓扑冗余，使得正向动力学推演计算成本过高。需要一种能够根据任务相关动力学复杂性进行自适应的降级方法。

Method: 提出PhySPRING，一种全可微的GNN方法。在每一降级层级中，合并具有相似学习动力学响应的节点以优化拓扑，同时保持每一降级层为显式弹簧-质点系统，联合学习粗化图拓扑和机械参数。

Result: 在PhysTwin基准上，PhySPRING比PhysTwin提高了密集重建和预测精度，降级模型在保持稳定物理和视觉保真度的同时实现高达2.30倍加速。在Real2Sim机器人策略评估中，降级模型零样本替换到ACT和π₀评估中，保持可比较的操作成功率并改善动作采样效率。

Conclusion: PhySPRING实现了高效且结构保持的弹簧-质点系统降级，在不牺牲保真度或机器人实用性的前提下提升了计算效率。

Abstract: Physics-based digital twins aim to predict the dynamics of real-world objects under interaction, enabling real-to-sim-to-real applications in robotics. Current approaches reconstruct such twins as explicit physical models (such as spring-mass systems) to predict the dynamics, but the resulting models often inherit the resolution of the visual reconstruction rather than being reduced to the physical complexity required to reproduce task-relevant dynamics. This mismatch introduces redundant topology, making repeated forward-dynamics rollouts unnecessarily expensive. To address this challenge, we present PhySPRING, an fully differentiable GNN-based method to reduce complexity in spring--mass digital twins. PhySPRING jointly learns a hierarchy of coarsened graph topologies and their mechanical parameters from observations. At each reduction level, PhySPRING merges nodes with similar learned dynamic responses to optimize the topology, while maintaining every reduced layer as an explicit spring--mass system. On the PhysTwin benchmark, PhySPRING improves dense reconstruction and prediction accuracy over PhysTwin, while reduced models retain stable physical and visual fidelity with up to a 2.30 times speed-up. We further demonstrate the effectiveness of PhySPRING in a Real2Sim robot policy-evaluation pipeline, where the reduced models are substituted zero-shot into ACT and $π_0$ evaluations, maintaining comparable manipulation success rates across downsampling levels while improving action-sampling effectiveness. Together, PhySPRING enables efficient and structure-preserving spring--mass reduction without sacrificing fidelity or robotic utility.

</details>


### [40] [Offline-Online Hierarchical 3D Global Relocalization With Synthetic LiDAR Sensing and Descriptor-Space Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07741)
*Jiahua Ren,Kai Shen,Muhua Zhang,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离-在线分层框架用于3D全局重定位，通过解耦搜索空间实现大规模环境下的高效重定位。离线阶段在栅格地图中模拟LiDAR扫描生成候选位置及几何描述子索引；在线阶段先通过全局检索获取粗略位姿，再进行点云配准输出精确6-DoF位姿。实际实验表明，该方法平均重定位时间3秒，定位精度8厘米，计算效率相比现有方法提升一个数量级，精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D全局重定位方法在大规模空间中面临在线重定位时间长的问题，主要源于庞大的位姿搜索空间和高计算开销。

Method: 提出离-在线分层框架：(1) 离线阶段在栅格地图中模拟LiDAR扫描生成候选位置及其几何描述子索引；(2) 在线阶段先通过全局检索获得粗略位姿估计，再通过点云配准输出精确6-DoF位姿。

Result: 真实环境实验中，平均重定位时间3秒，定位精度8厘米；相比现有全局重定位方法，计算效率提升一个数量级，重定位精度相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层框架通过解耦搜索空间有效解决了大规模3D环境中的全局重定位效率问题，实现了快速且准确的位姿估计。

Abstract: 3D global relocalization is one of the key capabilities for mobile robots in practical applications. However, in large scale spaces, existing methods often suffer from prolonged online relocalization time due to factors such as the massive pose search space and high computational overhead. To address these issues, this paper proposes an offline-online hierarchical framework that decouples the search space. In the offline phase, candidate positions and their corresponding geometric descriptor indices are generated in the map by simulating LiDAR scans within the grid map. In the online phase, a coarse pose estimate is first obtained via global retrieval, followed by point cloud registration to output precise 6-DoF pose estimates. Real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves an average relocalization time of 3 s and an average localization accuracy of 8 cm in 3D environments. Compared with existing global relocalization methods, the proposed method achieves an order-of-magnitude improvement in computational efficiency while delivering comparable relocalization accuracy.

</details>


### [41] [Sensitivity-Based Robust NMPC for Close-Proximity Offshore Wind Turbine Inspection with a Tilted Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07771)
*Giuseppe Silano,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于灵敏度分析的鲁棒NMPC方法，通过在线约束收紧来增强倾斜多旋翼无人机在近海风力涡轮机近距离检测任务中对塔筒安全距离的保持能力，在500次蒙特卡洛测试中完全消除了名义NMPC的碰撞风险，仅以适中的计算时间增加为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近距离海上风力涡轮机检测需在风扰和模型失配条件下对大型圆柱结构进行严格的间隙控制，而名义NMPC在质量、惯量、推力效率、阻力或风况偏离名义假设时可能违反安全约束。

Method: 提出基于灵敏度的鲁棒NMPC：利用一阶参数状态灵敏度提供结构化不确定性裕度，同时通过阶段相关加性裕度处理阵风扰动。该方法仅对名义NMPC进行灵敏度传播和裕度评估的增强，不改变滚动时域优化结构。

Result: 在边界关键的螺旋检测轨迹上进行500次不确定性实现的蒙特卡洛评估，结果表明所提控制器完全消除了名义NMPC下观察到的间隙违规，求解时间仅有适度增加。

Conclusion: 基于灵敏度分析的鲁棒NMPC能在不改变优化框架的前提下有效增强倾斜多旋翼在复杂风场环境下的安全间隙控制，兼顾了安全性与计算效率。

Abstract: Close-proximity offshore wind turbine inspection requires strict clearance control around large cylindrical structures under wind and model mismatch. Nominal Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) may violate safety constraints when mass, inertia, thrust effectiveness, drag, or wind conditions differ from nominal assumptions. We propose a sensitivity-based robust NMPC for a tilted multirotor that robustifies the tower-clearance constraint via online constraint tightening. First-order parametric state sensitivities provide a structured-uncertainty margin, while bounded gusts are handled by a stage-dependent additive margin. The formulation augments the nominal NMPC with sensitivity propagation and margin evaluation only, leaving the receding-horizon optimization structure unchanged. Monte-Carlo evaluation over 500 uncertainty realizations on a boundary-critical helical inspection trajectory shows that the proposed controller eliminates the clearance violations observed under nominal NMPC at the cost of a moderate increase in solve time.

</details>


### [42] [Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07835)
*Ethan Schneider,Jingkai Chen,Tianyi Gu,Kunlei Lian,Seth Hutchinson,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对多对多（many-to-many）MAPD问题提出M2M算法，该算法利用SKU标识符处理任务分配中的四维指派问题。实验表明，在8小时仓库运营模拟中，M2M及其变体M2M-wSKU相比现有方法，在不同环境和库存密度下平均多完成多达22,000个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPD研究多聚焦于一对一（one-to-one）任务变体，但真实仓库中物品可由多个位置存取（多对多场景），这构成NP难的四维指派问题，现有方法无法高效求解。

Method: 提出M2M算法，包含两个变体：M2M（最小化预估任务时长）和M2M-wSKU（将SKU分布纳入目标函数）。算法将多对多MAPD建模为四维分配问题并求解。

Result: 在8小时仓库运营模拟中，M2M在多种环境和库存密度下一致优于或持平先前最优方法，平均多完成高达22,000个任务。

Conclusion: M2M算法有效解决了多对多MAPD问题，通过SKU感知的任务分配显著提升了仓库自动化系统的任务吞吐量。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems in automated warehouses must manage continuous streams of pickup-and-delivery tasks while ensuring efficiency and safety. Prior work on Multi-Agent Pickup-and-Delivery (MAPD) has largely focused on the one-to-one variant, where each task has a fixed pickup and delivery location. In contrast, real warehouses often present many-to-many MAPD scenarios, where items, tracked by stock keeping unit (SKU) identifiers, can be retrieved from or stored at multiple locations, resulting in an NP-hard four-dimensional assignment problem. To solve the many-to-many MAPD problem, we contribute our algorithm: Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (M2M). We experiment with two variants of our algorithm: one that minimizes estimated task durations (M2M), and one which incorporates SKU distribution into the objective function (M2M-wSKU). Simulation results over 8-hour warehouse operations show that our method consistently matches or outperforms prior state of the art, with M2M completing up to 22,000 more tasks on average across different environments and warehouse inventory densities.

</details>


### [43] [Melding LLM and temporal logic for reliable human-swarm collaboration in complex scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07877)
*Junfeng Chen,Yuxiao Zhu,An Zhuo,Xintong Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Guanghui Wen,Xiwang Dong,Meng Guo,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种神经符号框架，通过结合时间逻辑规范、任务自动机、LLM推理和不确定性感知调度，实现了动态环境下长期人-机器人协作的可验证任务规划，显著降低操作员认知负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based规划工具生成的计划容易产生无效任务排序和不可执行动作，需要频繁人工调整；动态场景下的长期任务规划难以维持可靠性，且持续的操作员监督带来沉重认知负担。

Method: 1) 将任务目标和操作规则形式化为时间逻辑公式，将可允许任务排序形式化为任务自动机；2) LLMs在这些形式约束和实时感知上下文中生成可执行子任务序列；3) 不确定性感知调度器在异构群间分配子任务以最大化并行性和抗干扰能力；4) 事件触发交互协议将操作员参与限制为稀疏的高层确认和指导。

Result: 在异构机器人编队上部署获得了相似的结果，同时对硬件特定的执行和通信不确定性保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态环境下可靠、低负担的人-机器人协作提供了一种可扩展的正式范式。

Abstract: Robot swarms promise scalable assistance in complex and hazardous environments. Task planning lies at the core of human-swarm collaboration, translating the operator's intent into coordinated swarm actions and helping determine when validation or intervention is required during execution. In long-horizon missions under dynamic scenarios, however, reliable task planning becomes difficult to maintain: emerging events and changing conditions demand continual adaptation, and sustained operator oversight imposes substantial cognitive burden. Existing LLM-based planning tools can support plan generation, yet they remain susceptible to invalid task orderings and infeasible robot actions, resulting in frequent manual adjustment. Here we introduce a neuro-symbolic framework for long-horizon human-swarm collaboration that tightly melds verifiable task planning with context-grounded LLM reasoning. We formalize mission goals and operational rules as temporal logic formulas and admissible task orderings as task automata. Conditioned on these formal constraints and live perceptual context, LLMs generate executable subtask sequences that satisfy mission rules and remain grounded in the current scene. An uncertainty-aware scheduler then assigns subtasks across the heterogeneous swarm to maximize parallelisms while remaining resilient to disruptions. An event-triggered interaction protocol further limits operator involvement to sparse, high-level confirmation and guidance. Deployment on a heterogeneous robotic fleet yields similar results while remaining robust to hardware-specific actuation and communication uncertainties. Together, these results support a formal and scalable paradigm for reliable and low-overhead human-swarm collaboration in dynamic environments

</details>


### [44] [AERO-VIS: Asynchronous Event-based Real-time Onboard Visual-Inertial SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07885)
*Yannick Burkhardt,Sebastián Barbas Laina,Simon Boche,Leonard Freißmuth,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AERO-VIS是一个立体事件-惯性SLAM系统，采用异步事件流处理和数据驱动的关键点检测器，实现了首个纯事件基惯性SLAM系统，可在无人机上完成闭环控制和大规模状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高动态范围和抗运动模糊的优势，但现有事件基里程计方法大多以固定速率运行，限制了延迟和吞吐量。本文旨在开发一个异步处理的、低延迟的事件-惯性SLAM系统。

Method: 提出AERO-VIS系统，集成数据驱动的鲁棒关键点检测器；采用异步事件流处理方式，动态适应下游运行时需求；基于立体事件相机和惯性测量单元融合。

Result: 在无人机上实现了创纪录的机载事件基SLAM精度；首次展示纯事件基惯性SLAM系统实现闭环无人机控制和大规模状态估计。

Conclusion: AERO-VIS通过异步事件处理和优化关键点检测，在低延迟和实时性能方面取得突破，证明了纯事件基SLAM在无人机自主导航中的可行性。

Abstract: The robustness of event cameras to high dynamic range and motion blur holds the potential to improve visual odometry systems in challenging environments. Although their high temporal resolution does not require synchronous processing, most event-based odometry methods still run at fixed rates, which simplifies system design but restricts latency and throughput. In this work, we present AERO-VIS, a stereo event-inertial SLAM system with an integrated, data-driven, robust, and performance-optimized keypoint detector. By processing the event stream asynchronously, the system dynamically adapts to downstream runtime demands, ensuring low-latency and real-time performance. When deploying AERO-VIS on a UAV, we achieve unprecedented accuracy in onboard event-based SLAM. These unique characteristics enable us to present the first purely event-based inertial SLAM system that demonstrates closed-loop UAV control and large-scale state estimation while relying solely on onboard compute. A video of the experiments and the source code are available at ethz-mrl.github.io/AERO-VIS.

</details>


### [45] [TAVIS: A Benchmark for Egocentric Active Vision and Anticipatory Gaze in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07943)
*Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAVIS是一个面向主动视觉模仿学习的标准化评估基准，包含两个任务套件（TAVIS-Head和TAVIS-Hands）、三种评估原语（配对头/固定相机协议、GALT指标、过程性ID/OOD划分），并在两个类人机器人平台上验证了主动视觉的收益具有任务条件性、多任务策略在分布偏移下性能退化、以及模仿学习能自发产生预期性注视行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前主动视觉在模仿学习中虽有多个独立系统展示其优势，但缺乏统一的基准来比较不同方法、量化主动视觉在何种任务类型和条件下发挥作用。

Method: 构建TAVIS评估基础设施，包括：(1) 两个互补任务套件——TAVIS-Head（5个任务，通过pan/tilt颈部进行全局搜索）和TAVIS-Hands（3个任务，通过腕部相机处理局部遮挡）；(2) 三种评估原语——配对头相机vs固定相机协议、GALT（Gaze-Action Lead Time）指标、以及过程性ID/OOD划分；(3) 在GR1T2和Reachy2两个类人机器人平台上部署，基于IsaacLab构建。

Result: 基线实验（Diffusion Policy和π₀）表明：(i) 主动视觉整体上有帮助，但收益具有任务条件性而非统一提升；(ii) 多任务策略在受控分布偏移下性能急剧下降；(iii) 仅通过模仿学习就能产生预期性注视行为，其中位注视领先时间与人类遥操作员参考相当。

Conclusion: TAVIS提供了一个标准化基准，可系统评估主动视觉在模仿学习中的作用，并揭示了主动视觉收益的任务条件性、分布偏移下多任务策略的脆弱性，以及模仿学习自发学习预期性注视的能力。

Abstract: Active vision -- where a policy controls its own gaze during manipulation -- has emerged as a key capability for imitation learning, with multiple independent systems demonstrating its benefits in the past year. Yet there is no shared benchmark to compare approaches or quantify what active vision contributes, on which task types, and under what conditions. We introduce TAVIS, evaluation infrastructure for active-vision imitation learning, with two complementary task suites -- TAVIS-Head (5 tasks, global search via pan/tilt necks) and TAVIS-Hands (3 tasks, local occlusion via wrist cameras) -- on two humanoid torso embodiments (GR1T2, Reachy2), built on IsaacLab. TAVIS provides three evaluation primitives: a paired headcam-vs-fixedcam protocol on identical demonstrations; GALT (Gaze-Action Lead Time), a novel metric grounded in cognitive science and HRI that quantifies anticipatory gaze in learned policies; and procedural ID/OOD splits. Baseline experiments with Diffusion Policy and $π_0$ reveal that (i) active-vision generally helps, but benefits are task-conditional rather than uniform; (ii) multi-task policies degrade sharply under controlled distribution shifts on both suites; and (iii) imitation alone yields anticipatory gaze, with median lead times comparable to the human teleoperator reference. Code, evaluation scripts, demonstrations (LeRobot v3.0; ~2200 episodes) and trained baselines are released at https://github.com/spiglerg/tavis and https://huggingface.co/tavis-benchmark.

</details>


### [46] [Evaluation of an Actuated Spine in Agile Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07988)
*Nico Bohlinger,Piotr Kicki,Davide Tateo,Krzysztof Walas,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过仿真实验研究了四足机器人Silver Badger的1自由度驱动脊柱在多种敏捷运动场景（高速奔跑、爬楼梯、爬陡坡、跨障、匍匐）中的优势，结果表明驱动脊柱能显著提升机器人的敏捷性和越障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊椎在四足动物的动态运动中起着关键作用，能够提升步态的稳定性、速度和效率。作者希望探索将驱动脊柱引入四足机器人能否带来类似的性能提升，从而扩展机器人的运动能力。

Method: 在MuJoCo仿真环境中，以MAB Robotics公司的Silver Badger机器人为平台，配备矢状面1自由度驱动脊柱，在高速奔跑、爬楼梯、爬陡坡、跨障、匍匐五种场景下进行实证对比研究。

Result: 使用驱动脊柱的机器人展现出更高的敏捷性，能够克服更高的台阶、更陡的斜坡、更高的障碍物以及更小的通道。

Conclusion: 驱动脊柱对于提升四足机器人敏捷运动能力具有显著正向作用，是增强机器人运动性能的有效途径。

Abstract: The spine plays a crucial role in the dynamic locomotion of quadrupedal animals, improving the stability, speed, and efficiency of their gait, especially for fast-paced and highly agile movements. Therefore, the spine is also a promising and natural way to extend the capabilities of quadruped robots. This paper empirically investigates the benefits of an actuated spine for learning agile quadruped locomotion. We evaluate whether the use of the spine brings benefits in terms of high-speed running, climbing stairs, climbing high-angle slopes, hurdling, and crawling scenarios. We conducted an empirical study in MuJoCo simulation using the Silver Badger robot from MAB Robotics with an actuated 1-DOF spine in the sagittal plane. The obtained results show that the use of the spine provides the robot with increased agility and allows it to overcome higher stairs, steeper slopes, higher obstacles, and smaller passages.

</details>


### [47] [Active Embodiment Identification with Reinforcement Learning for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08020)

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于腿式机器人的主动具身识别方法，通过联合学习信息搜寻行为和显式具身预测，利用历史增强URMA架构在不同形态的仿真环境中推断关节级和全局具身参数。

Method: 采用历史增强URMA（Unsupervised Robot Morphology Adaptation）架构，联合学习信息搜寻行为（主动探索策略）和显式具身预测（关节级与全局参数估计），在仿真环境中与不同形态的机器人进行交互训练。

Result: 该方法能有效推断腿式机器人的关节级和全局具身参数，实现了主动信息获取与形态感知的联合学习。

Conclusion: 通过主动交互与环境反馈，机器人可以自主识别自身形态参数，为自适应控制与跨形态迁移提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present an active embodiment identification method for legged robots that jointly learns information-seeking behavior and explicit embodiment prediction. Using a history-augmented URMA architecture, the method infers joint-level and global embodiment parameters through interaction with the environment in simulation across different morphologies.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强力数构成的三项等差数列的存在性，并回应 Erdős 关于强力数序列连续三项是否为等差数列的提问。

Method: 通过构造特定公差 d = 2√N + 1 的三项等差数列，证明存在无穷多个这样的数列由强力数组成。

Conclusion: 猜想这些等差数列中无穷多个是强力数序列中的连续三项，若成立则将否定 Erdős 的相关问题。

Abstract: We show that infinitely many three-term arithmetic progressions $N, N+d, N+2d$ of powerful numbers exist with $d = 2\sqrt{N} + 1$. We further conjecture that infinitely many of these progressions consist of three consecutive terms in the sequence of powerful numbers, which would answer a question of Erdős in the negative.

</details>


### [49] [On almost primes solutions to forms of odd degrees in many variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06942)
*Akos Magyar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了由奇数次多项式方程组定义的解中每个分量均为素数乘有界整数的解的数量下界，证明了当变量数足够大时，存在至少 \(C_\mathcal{F} N^{s-D}/(\log N)^s\) 个这样的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多项式方程组在素数分量约束下的整数解的存在性与计数问题，这是解析数论与丢番图几何交叉领域的基础问题。

Method: 通过将变量分解为 \(x_i = y_i p_i\) 的形式，其中 \(|y_i| \leq Y_\mathcal{F}\) 且 \(p_i\) 为素数，利用圆法或筛法结合代数几何中的维数估计，在变量数 \(s\) 充分大于参数 \(R,d\) 的条件下证明解的下界。

Result: 证明了当变量数 \(s\) 相对于 \(R,d\) 足够大时，存在至少 \(C_\mathcal{F} N^{s-D}/(\log N)^s\) 个满足条件的解，其中常数 \(C_\mathcal{F}>0\)，\(D\) 仅依赖于 \(R,d\)。

Conclusion: 在奇数次多项式方程组情形下，素数分量解的计数下界与经典整数解情形仅相差对数因子，且维数损失 \(D\) 仅与方程组的度数和方程个数有关，与具体系数无关。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{F}=\{f_1,\ldots,f_R\}$ be a family of forms of odd degrees at most $d$ in $s$ variables. We study the solutions to the system $f_1(\mathbf{x})=\ldots=f_R(\mathbf{x})=0$ of the form $x_i=y_ip_i$ with $|y_i|\leq Y_\mathcal{F}$ and $p_i$ being a prime for all $i\in [s]$ inside the box $[-N,N]^s$, for large $N$. We show that if the number of variables $s$ is sufficiently large with respect to the parameters $R$ and $d$, then there are at least $C_\mathcal{F} N^{s-D}/(\log\,N)^s$ such solutions for some constants $C_\mathcal{F}>0$ and $D\in\mathbb{N}$, with $D$ depending only on the initial parameters $R$ and $d$.

</details>


### [50] [Poissonian correlations of $αn^d$ mod $1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06974)
*Chris Lutsko,Nick Rome,Niclas Technau*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究序列 x(n)=αn^d mod 1 的ℓ点关联，证明当d足够大时，对几乎所有α具有泊松关联性，并利用傅里叶分析传递原理证明了在Hausdorff维数满集的恶劣逼近α上也成立。证明关键是通过行列式方法计数对角超曲面上的点，利用特殊解随d增长占主导的特性进行分层计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Berry-Tabor猜想以及Rudnick-Sarnak猜想关于序列点关联性质的预测，研究幂函数序列模1分布的相关结构。

Method: 1) 应用行列式方法计数对角超曲面上的点；2) 对解进行分层，区分属于低维子簇的"特殊解"和其他"非特殊解"；3) 证明当d增大时特殊解占主导，非特殊解变得极其稀少；4) 利用傅里叶分析传递原理将结果推广到恶劣逼近α集合。

Result: 1) 当d足够大时，对几乎所有α，序列具有泊松ℓ点关联；2) 存在一组Hausdorff维数满集的恶劣逼近α，该序列也具有泊松ℓ点关联。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了Berry-Tabor猜想在该类序列中的预期行为，同时推进了Rudnick-Sarnak猜想的相关研究，展示了分层计数方法在处理高维对角超曲面点计数问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Let $x(n):=αn^d \mod 1$ for integer $d >1$ and non-zero real $α$. We show that $\{x(n)\}_{n>0}$ has Poissonian $\ell$-point correlations for almost all choices of $α$ when $d$ is large (depending on $\ell$). This falls in line with the expected behavior from the Berry--Tabor conjecture. Further, in the spirit of a conjecture of Rudnick--Sarnak, we show Poissonian $\ell$-point correlations for a set of badly approximable $α$ of full Hausdorff dimension by a Fourier analytic transference principle.
  The proof makes use of an application of the determinant method to count points on a diagonal hypersurface of degree $d$ in such a way as to capture the contribution of points belonging to lower dimensional varieties. As $d$ grows, these `special solutions' dominate the count and non-special solutions become increasingly rare. This stratified counting statement allows us to control the number of points on average very effectively.

</details>


### [51] [Anderson generating function of rank-one Drinfeld Module over rational function fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07484)
*Chuangqiang Hu,Xiao-Min Huang,Stephen S. -T. Yau*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在秩1 Drinfeld模算术中取得根本性突破，推导出在积分环A上的显式公式，将经典多项式环推广到具有无穷远点次数N≥2的射影直线。构建了Anderson生成函数，揭示了与Carlitz理论的关键区别，并引入指数作用解决Galois群作用导致的障碍，同时推广到任意Dedekind整环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补了正特征算术中无穷远点次数>1情形下Drinfeld模理论长期存在的空白，建立与经典Carlitz模理论相平行的完整框架。

Method: 构造Anderson生成函数，通过Pellarin级数、指数扭模和对数形变将其与Carlitz周期联系；引入指数作用处理Galois群作用导致的障碍；利用Drinfeld模的对偶修正留数公式。

Result: 发现标准Anderson生成函数留数公式因Galois群作用而失效的关键区别；通过指数作用实现对全体扭曲指数函数的同步研究；建立了修正的留数公式。

Conclusion: 本文方法可自然推广到任意Dedekind整环，为Drinfeld模理论开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We establish a fundamental breakthrough in rank-one Drinfeld module arithmetic by deriving explicit formulas over the integral domain $\A = H^{0}(\mathbb{P}^1-P_ρ, \mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1})$, which generalizes the classical polynomial ring ($N=1$) to the projective line associated with an infinite place of degree $N \geqslant 2$. This fills a longstanding gap by developing a comprehensive parallel to Carlitz module theory foundational in positive characteristic arithmetic for the understudied case of infinite places of degree $>1$. We construct Anderson generating functions for these modules and link them to the Carlitz period via Pellarin's series, exponential torsion modules, and logarithmic deformations. These constructions provide powerful tools for studying such Drinfeld modules and their associated $L$-series, central to modern number theory. A key result reveals a critical distinction from Carlitz theory: the standard Anderson generating function residue formula fails due to Galois group action. We resolve this obstruction by introducing an exponential action, enabling simultaneous study of all twisted exponential functions a major methodological advance. We further show that Anderson generating function computation involves the dual of Drinfeld modules, leading to an appropriate residue formula modification. Abstract: We prove a volume-uniform effective-Hamiltonian theorem for bounded finite-range quantum spin systems on possibly infinite lattices. Abstract: In recent work, we initiated a research program aimed at the systematic investigation of quantum superintegrable systems describing the interaction of two non-relativistic spin-$1/2$ particles in three-dimensional Euclidean space. In that study, we classified all such superintegrable systems admitting additional first-order scalar integrals of motion. In the present paper, we continue this program by focusing on systems that admit additional pseudo-scalar integrals of motion. Starting from the most general rotationally invariant Hamiltonian for two interacting spin-$1/2$ particles, we construct the most general first-order pseudo-scalar operator in the form of a matrix polynomial in the momenta. Imposing the commutativity of this operator with the Hamiltonian leads to a system of determining equations. By solving these equations, we obtain a complete classification of such superintegrable systems and determine the corresponding pseudo-scalar integrals of motion. The resulting classification provides new families of superintegrable systems with spin-dependent interactions. These systems enrich the class of integrable models relevant to nucleon--nucleon interactions and contribute to the broader program of classifying superintegrable quantum systems with spin. Abstract: We studied the constrained Hamiltonian formulation of a supersymmetric Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) equation, which is observed to be a constrained system similar to its classical version. The corresponding degenerate Lagrangian requires an exclusive consideration and the utilization of the Dirac-Bergmann algorithm. We explicitly determined the full set of primary and secondary constraints and constructed the total Hamiltonian governing the dynamics of the system. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器收集的(2712.4±14.3)×10^6 psi(3686)事例，首次联合测量了Xi(1530)^- → Xi^0 π^- 和 Xi(1530)^- → Xi^- π^0 的绝对分支比，在同位旋对称性假设下将两个衰变模式视为完全关联，结果分别为(61.4±4.5±4.6)%和(29.7±2.2±2.2)%，组合分支比为(91.1±6.7±6.8)%。同时更新了psi(3686) → anti-Xi^+ Xi(1530)^- + c.c.的分支比为(8.67±0.52±0.58±0.57)×10^{-6}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Xi(1530)^-的绝对分支比尚未被实验直接测量，需要通过理论假设或间接方法推断。本文旨在利用大量psi(3686)数据，通过psi(3686) → anti-Xi^+ Xi(1530)^- + c.c.过程，首次直接测量Xi(1530)^-两个主要衰变模式的绝对分支比，以检验同位旋对称性并精确确定其衰变性质。

Method: 使用BESIII探测器收集的约27.12亿psi(3686)事例，通过psi(3686) → anti-Xi^+ Xi(1530)^- + c.c.过程产生Xi(1530)^-，联合分析其两个衰变通道Xi(1530)^- → Xi^0 π^- 和 Xi(1530)^- → Xi^- π^0。在同位旋对称性假设下，将两个衰变模式作为完全相关处理，进行拟合提取分支比。系统误差通过考虑事件选择、拟合模型和干涉效应等因素进行评估。

Result: 首次测量得到：B(Xi(1530)^- → Xi^0 π^-) = (61.4 ± 4.5 ± 4.6)%，B(Xi(1530)^- → Xi^- π^0) = (29.7 ± 2.2 ± 2.2)%，组合分支比B(Xi(1530)^- → (Xi π)^-) = (91.1 ± 6.7 ± 6.8)%。更新psi(3686) → anti-Xi^+ Xi(1530)^- + c.c.的分支比为(8.67 ± 0.52 ± 0.58 ± 0.57)×10^{-6}。

Conclusion: 该工作首次直接测量了Xi(1530)^-的绝对分支比，结果与同位旋对称性的预期一致，并提供了更精确的psi(3686)衰变到anti-Xi^+ Xi(1530)^-的分支比更新值。这些测量为理解重子激发态性质及强相互作用动力学提供了重要实验依据。

Abstract: Based on (2712.4+-14.3)*10^{6} psi(3686) events collected with the BESIII detector, the decays Xi(1530)^{-} to Xi^{0} pi^{-} and Xi(1530)^{-} to Xi^{-} pi^{0} are investigated jointly via the process psi(3686) to anti-Xi^{+} Xi(1530)^{-} + c.c. Under the assumption of isospin symmetry, the two decay modes are treated as fully correlated, and we report the first measurement of their absolute branching fractions. The results are B(Xi(1530)^{-} to Xi^{0} pi^{-})=(61.4+-4.5+-4.6)% and B(Xi(1530)^{-} to Xi^{-} pi^{0}) =(29.7+-2.2+-2.2)%. The combined branching fraction of the two decays is B(Xi(1530)^{-} to (Xi pi)^{-})=(91.1+-6.7+-6.8)%, with uncertainties accounting for the correlations between the two modes. Here, the first uncertainties are statistical, while the second are systematic. Additionally, we update the branching fraction of the decay psi(3686) to anti-Xi^{+} Xi(1530)^{-} + c.c. The updated measurement is B(psi(3686) to anti-Xi^{+} Xi(1530)^{-} + c.c.)=(8.67+-0.52+-0.58+-0.57)*10^{-6}, where the first uncertainty is statistical, the second is systematic related to event selection and the fit model, and the third is associated with the interference effect.

</details>


### [62] [Search for Sub-GeV Axion-Like Particles at EBES Pilot Run Using 4 GeV Positron Beam at KEK LINAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07108)
*Takahiro Fusayasu,Tomoya Iizawa,Fumihito Ikeda,Akimasa Ishikawa,Masako Iwasaki,Hiroshi Iwase,Takahiro Kawahara,Aoi Masaki,Fusashi Miyahara,Yu Morikawa,Yuichi Okayasu,Toshiyuki Ono,Hidetoshi Otono,Yasuhito Sakaki,Takumi Seino,Yuta Shimasaki,Taikan Suehara,Yosuke Takubo,Shusaku Tsumura,Kosuke Uemura,Yifu Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: EBES实验利用KEK LINAC 4 GeV正电子束流，通过Primakoff过程寻找亚GeV轴子-like粒子（ALP），在无背景信号区域未观测到事件，给出了90%置信水平的上限，拓展了参数空间覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索亚GeV质量区间的轴子-like粒子（ALP），填补现有实验在低质量-耦合参数空间中的空白。

Method: 使用4 GeV正电子束打靶钨束流dump，通过轫致辐射光子诱导Primakoff过程产生ALP，衰变道为a→γγ；采用PbO量热器探测信号，利用数据驱动方法估计本底，定义信号区域期望本底低于0.1事件。

Result: 揭盲后未观测到信号事件，在90%置信水平给出ALP质量-耦合平面上的上限，拓展了此前实验未覆盖的参数区间。

Conclusion: EBES pilot run对亚GeV ALP的搜索未发现信号，但成功将实验灵敏度延伸至新参数区域，证明了该实验方案的可行性。

Abstract: We report the results of a search for sub-GeV axion-like particles (ALPs) using pilot run data from the Electron Beam-dump Experiment at KEK LINAC Switching Yard 3 (EBES). The data were collected in December 2023 with a 4 GeV positron beam and correspond to $1.3\times10^{14}$ positrons on target. In the pilot run setup, a tungsten beam dump and a single PbO calorimeter were used. We consider ALP production via the Primakoff process induced by bremsstrahlung photons in the beam dump, followed by the decay $a\toγγ$. The background was estimated with a data-driven method, and a signal region was defined such that the expected background yield is below 0.1 events. No events were observed after unblinding. Upper limits at the 90% confidence level were derived in the ALP mass-coupling plane, extending the experimental coverage into a region of parameter space not explored by previous searches.

</details>

Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用BESIII探测器在7.33 fb⁻¹数据样本中，首次测量了D_s⁺→K*(892)⁰μ⁺ν_μ衰变的分支比，改进了电子通道的测量，并首次完成了D_s⁺→K*(892)⁰跃迁强子形状因子的完整动力学研究，检验了轻子普适性未发现违背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次测量D_s⁺→K*(892)⁰μ⁺ν_μ的衰变，改进电子通道测量，并完成该跃迁的完整动力学研究，为微扰QCD等非微扰理论计算提供严格检验。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的7.33 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻湮灭数据（质心能量4.128–4.226 GeV），同时研究两个半轻衰变道的动力学，提取形状因子参数并测量微分衰变率和轻子前后不对称性。

Result: 分支比B(D_s⁺→K*(892)⁰μ⁺ν_μ)=(2.07±0.22_stat±0.10_syst)×10⁻³，B(D_s⁺→K*(892)⁰e⁺ν_e)=(2.14±0.18_stat±0.10_syst)×10⁻³；形状因子参数r_V=1.63±0.14_stat±0.08_syst，r₂=0.60±0.13_stat±0.06_syst，A₁(0)=0.56±0.02_stat±0.01_syst；首次给出了模型无关的微分衰变率和轻子前后不对称性，轻子普适性检验未发现违背。

Conclusion: 首次完成了D_s⁺→K*(892)⁰跃迁的完整动力学研究，r_V和r₂的精度提高两倍，A₁(0)首次被测量，为理论计算提供了严格的实验约束。

Abstract: We report the first measurement of the semileptonic decay $D^+_s \rightarrow K^*(892)^0μ^+ν_μ$ and an improved measurement of the decay $D^+_s \rightarrow K^*(892)^0 e^+ν_{e}$ using a sample of $7.33~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ annihilation data collected at center-of-mass energies between 4.128 to 4.226~GeV with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider. We measure the branching fractions to be $\mathcal B({D^+_s\rightarrow K^*(892)^0 μ^+ν_μ})=(2.07\pm0.22_{\rm stat}\pm0.10_{\rm syst})\times10^{-3}$ and $\mathcal B({D^+_s\rightarrow K^*(892)^0 e^+ν_{e}})=(2.14\pm0.18_{\rm stat}\pm0.10_{\rm syst})\times10^{-3}$. Based on a simultaneous study of the dynamics in two semileptonic decays, the hadronic form factor parameters in the $D^+_s\rightarrow K^{*}(892)^0$ transition are determined to be $r_{V} = V(0)/A_1(0) = 1.63 \pm 0.14_{\rm stat} \pm 0.08_{\rm syst}$, $r_{2} = A_2(0)/A_1(0) = 0.60 \pm 0.13_{\rm stat} \pm 0.06_{\rm syst}$, and $A_1(0)=0.56 \pm 0.02_{\rm stat} \pm 0.01_{\rm syst}$, where $V(0)$ is the vector form factor and $A_{1,2}(0)$ are the axial-vector form factors evaluated at $q^2=0$. The precision of $r_V$ and $r_2$ is improved by twofold and $A_1(0)$ is measured for the first time. We also report the first model-independent measurements of the differential decay rates and the lepton forward-backward asymmetries for $D^+_s\rightarrow K^{*}(892)^0\ell^+ν_{\ell}$ decays. Based on these measurements, we perform a test of lepton flavor universality in full and separate $q^2$ intervals with $D^+_s\rightarrow K^{*}(892)^0\ell^+ν_{\ell}$ decays. No violation is found within uncertainties. Our results present for the first time a complete study of the dynamics in the $D_s^+\rightarrow K^*(892)^0$ transition, and provide stringent tests of various non-perturbative theoretical calculations.

</details>


### [64] [NEXT Simulation Dataset for AI Summer School UC Irvine 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07566)
*K. Mistry*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文档发布了来自NEXT探测器中$^{214}$Bi衰变产生的高压氙气模拟$0νββ$和本底事件数据集，该数据集用于2026年6月20-21日在加州大学尔湾分校举办的无中微子双贝塔衰变（$0νββ$）AI暑期学校。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为$0νββ$ AI暑期学校提供模拟数据集，用于训练和开发区分信号与本底的机器学习算法，推动无中微子双贝塔衰变研究的AI应用。

Method: 通过模拟高压氙气中$^{214}$Bi衰变产生的本底事件以及$0νββ$信号事件，生成类似于NEXT探测器产生的数据集，并将数据集公开发布。

Result: 成功产出了一套模拟$0νββ$信号与$^{214}$Bi本底事件的数据集，可用于AI/ML模型的训练与评估。

Conclusion: 该数据集发布将支持$0νββ$ AI暑期学校的教育活动，促进利用人工智能技术解决无中微子双贝塔衰变实验中信号-本底区分问题的研究。

Abstract: This document details the dataset release of simulated $0νββ$ and background events originating from the decay of $^{214}$Bi in high-pressure xenon gas, describing events similar to those produced in the NEXT detector. This release is part of the Neutrinoless Double Beta Decay ($0νββ$) AI Summer School held on June 20-21 2026 at the University of California, Irvine.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb实验利用8.4 fb$^{-1}$的质子-质子对撞数据，通过全角度分布分析$\overline{B}^0 \to \overline{K}^{*0}μ^+μ^-$衰变中的$C\!P$破坏，在弱有效理论框架下通过无分箱最大似然拟合确定复Wilson系数，将$C\!P$破坏观测量精度提升一个量级，未发现显著$C\!P$破坏，结果与标准模型一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在$\overline{B}^0 \to \overline{K}^{*0}μ^+μ^-$这一味改变中性流过程中搜索$C\!P$破坏效应，检验标准模型的预测。

Method: 利用LHCb Run 1和Run 2收集的8.4 fb$^{-1}$质子-质子对撞数据，对衰变的全角度分布进行分析；在弱有效理论框架下，通过无分箱最大似然拟合角度观测量确定复Wilson系数，并纳入非局域强子振幅对整个双缪子质量谱的贡献。

Result: $C\!P$破坏观测量精度较此前测量提升一个数量级，Wilson系数的虚部比实部确定得更精确；未观测到显著的$C\!P$破坏信号。

Conclusion: 结果与标准模型预测一致，未发现超出标准模型的新物理$C\!P$破坏迹象。

Abstract: A search for $C\!P$ violation in the $\overline{B}^0 \to \overline{K}^{*0}μ^+μ^-$ decay is performed using proton--proton collision data collected by the LHCb experiment during Run 1 and Run 2, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 8.4 fb$^{-1}$. The analysis exploits the full angular distribution of the decay, providing sensitivity to $C\!P$-violating effects in both vector and axial-vector contributions to this flavour-changing neutral-current process. The complex Wilson coefficients are determined within the Weak Effective Theory through an unbinned maximum-likelihood fit to the angular observables, incorporating nonlocal hadronic amplitudes across the full dimuon mass spectrum. The precision of the $C\!P$-violation observables is improved by an order of magnitude relative to previous measurements, with the imaginary parts of the Wilson coefficients now determined more precisely than the real parts. No significant $C\!P$ violation is observed, and the results are consistent with Standard Model.

</details>


### [66] [Evidence for the decay $B^0_s\toφη'$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07586)

Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于LHCb实验2011-2018年采集的9 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，以3.5σ显著性找到了B⁰_s→φη'衰变的证据，并测量了其相对分支比和绝对分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找B⁰_s→φη'稀有衰变，检验标准模型对CKM压制过程的理论预言。

Method: 利用LHCb实验在√s=7,8,13 TeV下采集的9 fb⁻¹数据，通过相对分支比方法以B⁰_s→φφ为基准道进行测量。

Result: 相对分支比R=(3.56±0.79±0.18±0.06)×10⁻²，对应B(B⁰_s→φη')=(0.66±0.15±0.03±0.02)×10⁻⁶，显著性3.5σ。

Conclusion: 首次为B⁰_s→φη'稀有衰变提供了3.5σ水平的证据，测量结果与标准模型预期一致。

Abstract: Using a dataset corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $9 \,\textrm{fb}^{-1}$ collected in proton-proton collisions between 2011 and 2018 by the LHCb experiment, evidence is found for the decay $B^0_s\toφη'$ with $3.5 σ$ significance. The branching ratio relative to the $B^0_s\toφφ$ decay is determined to be $R=(3.56 \pm 0.79\pm 0.18\pm 0.06)\times10^{-2}$. This corresponds to a branching fraction, $B(B^0_s\toφη')=(0.66 \pm 0.15 \pm 0.03 \pm 0.02) \times 10^{-6}$ where, in both cases, the first uncertainty is statistical, the second systematic, and the third due to external branching fractions.

</details>

### [67] [Nonlinear Amplification of Finite-Sample Uncertainty in Capability-Based Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06843)
*Fei Jiang,Lei Yang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限样本不确定性在非线性变换（如过程能力指数）下的传播机制，发现能力估计的微小误差会通过尾部曲率被不成比例地放大为缺陷风险指标（如缺陷概率和PPM），导致决策不稳定，并验证了这一机制对样本量要求和决策可靠性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计决策系统中常用非线性变换处理有限样本数据，但有限样本不确定性如何在这些变换下传播尚不清晰，现有方法未能充分解释能力指数估计稳定但缺陷风险评估波动大的现象。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示能力不确定性到缺陷风险的非线性放大机制，结合Monte Carlo仿真与工业数据集验证所提机制。

Result: 能力估计的微小误差在缺陷概率和PPM等指标上被显著放大，这种放大源于缺陷概率对尾曲率的敏感性；能力指数空间看似稳定，但缺陷风险空间波动剧烈；样本量要求与决策可靠性直接相关。

Conclusion: 提出了统一解释有限样本下决策不稳定性的机制，为可靠性感知决策框架提供了理论基础，并明确了分布假设对缺陷风险估计的实践影响。

Abstract: This paper studies the propagation of finite-sample uncertainty under nonlinear transformations commonly used in statistical decision systems. In particular, we consider process capability indices, which are widely used in manufacturing practice but are estimated from finite samples, rendering the resulting approval decisions inherently uncertain. We show that such uncertainty cannot be fully explained by estimator variability alone, but is substantially influenced by a nonlinear amplification mechanism through which capability uncertainty is transformed into defect-risk metrics. While capability estimators vary approximately linearly with process dispersion, defect probabilities depend on tail curvature, causing small estimation errors to be disproportionately amplified in measures such as defect probability and parts-per-million (PPM) rates. Consequently, capability assessments that appear stable in index space may exhibit substantial variability in defect-risk space, particularly near decision thresholds. This insight provides a unified explanation of finite-sample decision instability, motivates reliability-aware decision formulations, and links sample-size requirements directly to decision reliability. Monte Carlo simulations and industrial data analyses validate the proposed mechanism and demonstrate its practical implications, including the impact of distributional assumptions on defect-risk estimation.

</details>


### [68] [There to care; not to kill: medical settings, statistics and wrongful convictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07421)
*Richard D. Gill*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文探讨医疗环境中护士被错误定罪的案件特征，指出这类案件缺乏直接证据（如目击、DNA、监控、认罪），主要依靠统计关联、私人日记的曲解、法医证据的模糊性以及医院主管医生的推动，导致冤案发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示医疗领域护士被错误定罪的普遍特征，指出仅凭统计关联和间接推断定罪的危险性，引发对司法公正和医疗事故调查程序的反思。

Method: 通过归纳分析多起护士被错误定罪案件的共性特征，包括：缺乏直接证据（目击、DNA、监控、认罪）、缺乏既往精神病理行为证据、私人写作被曲解为认罪、日常行为被赋予恶意解读、动机纯属推测、主要依据统计关联（死亡或病情恶化与特定护士的关联）、法医证据模糊、调查受医院主管医生驱动等。

Result: 识别出护士被错误定罪案件的系统性模式：缺乏强直接证据，定罪依赖统计关联、曲解的私人记录、模糊的法医证据，且调查过程受与案件有利害关系的医院主管医生主导。

Conclusion: 医疗领域的错误定罪案件存在可识别的模式，应警惕仅凭统计关联和间接证据定罪的风险，需改革调查程序以避免冤案。

Abstract: This paper discusses wrongful convictions in a medical setting, focusing on nurses. Common features are lack of strong direct evidence: the nurse was never seen doing anything wrong. There is no DNA evidence of tampering of apparatus or medications by the nurse. There is no CCTV footage showing suspicious actions. Analysis of medical records at the time led coroners to issue certificates of natural deaths, and most events were not, at the time, thought suspicious by hospital staff. There is no confession and the nurse consistently asserts they are completely innocent. There is no evidence of earlier psychopathic behaviour. Instead, private writings (e.g., in a diary) are interpreted by the prosecution as a confession; mundane behaviour is given a sinister interpretation. Motive remains speculation. The main evidence is statistical: a spike in deaths or collapses and a statistical association with a particular nurse. There is forensic evidence which suggests one or two patients might have been harmed by administration of medication much used in the hospital, and even legitimately used earlier in the care of the alleged victims. Police investigations are driven by the hospital consultants who were clinically responsible for the patients allegedly killed or harmed by the nurse.

</details>

### [69] [Disentangling bulk and surface electronic structure using targeted cleave planes in RuO$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06798)
*Maria H. Visscher,Sebastian Buchberger,Bruno Saika,Shu Mo,Lea Richter,Mats Leandersson,Craig Polley,Andrew P. Mackenzie,Phil D. C. King*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过聚焦离子束（FIB）工程解理制备(110)和(100)表面，利用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）对RuO₂的电子结构进行了高质量测量。结果表明，ARPES谱主要由表面电子态主导，通过与密度泛函理论对比，区分了表面态、表面共振态和高度三维体态，并发现了表面区域由空间反演对称性破缺导致的显著Rashba型自旋劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO₂因其潜在的非传统电子和磁性质以及与超导电性的邻近性而备受关注，但其强三维晶体结构使得电子结构的测量和解释变得复杂。因此，需要一种有效方法获取高质量的电子结构测量数据。

Method: 采用聚焦离子束（FIB）工程解理技术制备(110)和(100)表面，结合角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）测量电子结构，并与密度泛函理论（DFT）计算进行对比分析。

Result: ARPES谱主要被表面电子态主导；表面态呈现依赖于表面终止的变异性；成功区分了表面态、表面共振态与高度三维体态；在表面区域发现Ru 4d轨道显著的Rashba型自旋劈裂，归因于空间反演对称性破缺和强自旋轨道耦合。

Conclusion: FIB工程解理制备特定表面是获取RuO₂高质量ARPES测量的有效手段；RuO₂的表面电子态对其电子结构有重要贡献，且表面区域存在显著的自旋轨道耦合效应。

Abstract: Rutile RuO$_2$ has attracted significant interest due to its putative unconventional electronic and magnetic properties and its proximity to superconductivity. However, the measurement and interpretation of its electronic structure has been complicated by a strongly three-dimensional crystal structure. Here, we demonstrate how the preparation of targeted $(110)$ and $(100)$ surfaces via focused ion beam (FIB)-engineered cleaving allows the acquisition of high-quality measurements of the electronic structure using angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. Our results demonstrate that ARPES spectra of RuO$_2$ are, in fact, largely dominated by signatures of distinct surface electronic states. From comparison with density-functional theory, we resolve a surface termination-dependent variation of these, and disentangle them from highly-three-dimensional bulk states and surface resonances. Moreover, we find a marked role of the substantial spin-orbit coupling of the Ru 4$d$ orbitals in the surface region, where a breaking of spatial inversion symmetry leads to significant Rashba-type spin splittings of the surface bands.

</details>


### [70] [Does a Fractional Quantum Hall Edge Have a Protected Intrinsic Dipole Moment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06801)
*Domagoj Perković,Konstantinos Vasiliou,S. A. Parameswaran,Steven H. Simon*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过DMRG方法重新检验了Park和Haldane关于分数量子霍尔系统边缘电偶极矩固有值的论断，发现仅在ν=1/3边缘存在该固有值，而ν=2/3和Pfaffian/反Pfaffian界面均不支持该论断，并基于复合费米子理论解释了层级态通常不具有受保护固有偶极矩的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Park和Haldane提出的FQH系统物理边缘存在受保护固有电偶极矩值的论断，解决此前数值研究中的局限性和争议。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重正化群(DMRG)方法精确计算基态偶极矩，选取ν=1/3-真空边缘、ν=2/3-真空边缘以及Pfaffian和反Pfaffian相之间的界面三个代表性系统进行研究。

Result: 仅在ν=1/3边缘观察到预期的固有偶极矩值，而ν=2/3边缘和Pfaffian/反Pfaffian界面均不表现出该固有值。

Conclusion: 基于复合费米子理论，层级态通常不具有受保护的固有偶极矩；该结果对FQH态的边缘能量学和边缘结构具有重要影响。

Abstract: We investigate the claims by Park and Haldane [Phys. Rev. B 90, 045123 (2014)] of an intrinsic protected value of the electric dipole moment at the physical edge of fractional quantum Hall (FQH) systems. Contrary to prevailing expectations, we find that the edge dipole takes the expected intrinsic value only in certain very special cases. We identify key limitations in earlier numerical studies and employ density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) methods to accurately compute the ground-state dipole. We focus on three representative systems: the $ν=1/3$-vacuum edge, the $ν=2/3$-vacuum edge, and the interface between Pfaffian and anti-Pfaffian phases. We find that the expected intrinsic dipole value occurs only at $ν=1/3$, whereas the other systems do not exhibit the claimed intrinsic value. We give arguments based on composite fermions as to why hierarchy states should generally not have protected intrinsic dipoles. These results have important implications for the energetics and edge structure of FQH states.

</details>


### [71] [Fluctuation-driven chiral ferromagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06852)
*Rokas Veitas,Ahmed Khalifa,Francisco Machado,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明自旋轨道耦合（非磁化守恒相互作用）能在铁磁材料中通过量子涨落稳定手性铁磁态，包括具有大轨道手性的铁磁体和手性条纹相，这些手性态具有增强的热霍尔效应等输运特征，对莫尔异质结构、里德伯原子阵列等系统有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁材料中量子涨落通常被抑制，限制了可观测现象的范围；作者希望证明非磁化守恒耦合能改变这一范式，在手性铁磁体中通过量子涨落稳定新奇的相。

Method: 通过引入自旋轨道相互作用（非磁化守恒耦合），修改经典相图；经典分析仅预测共线态，而考虑量子涨落后观察到涨落稳定的新相。

Result: 发现了涨落稳定的手性铁磁相（具有大轨道手性）和手性条纹相；这些手性态自发产生标量轨道手性，区别于传统依赖场致矢量手性倾斜的机制；并展现出增强的热霍尔效应。

Abstract: In general, quantum fluctuations are suppressed in ferromagnetic materials because they admit a simple unfrustrated ground state, greatly limiting the scope of phenomena that can be observed in these materials. In this work, we show how magnetization-non-conserving couplings fundamentally alter this paradigm by demonstrating the existence of a chiral ferromagnet that is stabilized by quantum fluctuations. More specifically, we show how these spin-orbit interactions modify the classical phase diagram; whereas a classical analysis predicts only collinear states, we observe fluctuation-stabilized phases, including a ferromagnet with large orbital chirality and a chiral stripe regime. Result: ME响应由s-p杂化激活的轨道自由度主导，自旋贡献因自旋守恒而消失。低能有效模型中直接使用Kubo公式会失败，但通过系统引入轨道杂化相关的顶点修正可解决此问题。重整化Kubo公式能准确复现多轨道结果。

Abstract: Magnetoelectric (ME) effects in antiferromagnets provide a fertile platform for exploring symmetry-driven cross-correlated responses. However, their microscopic origin remains elusive and is often obscured in simplified low-energy descriptions. In this study, we revisit the microscopic mechanism of the ME effect in a collinear antiferromagnetic zigzag chain by employing a multi-orbital tight-binding model that explicitly includes both $s$- and $p$-orbital degrees of freedom. Using analytical and numerical calculations based on the Kubo formula, we demonstrate that the ME response is governed by orbital degrees of freedom activated through $s$--$p$ hybridization, while the spin contribution vanishes due to spin conservation. To elucidate the low-energy description, we derive an effective Hamiltonian projected onto the $s$-orbital subspace using the Schur complement. We show that a naive application of the Kubo formula within this effective model fails to capture the ME response. This issue is resolved by systematically incorporating vertex corrections in terms of orbital hybridization into the response functions. Furthermore, by introducing a quasiparticle renormalization scheme, we formulate a renormalized Kubo formula that preserves conservation laws and accurately reproduces the full multi-orbital results. Our analysis revisits the conventional low-energy perspective and reveals that the ME effect originates from virtual interorbital processes encoded in vertex corrections, rather than from the bare low-energy Hamiltonian. The effective framework developed here provides a unified microscopic understanding of orbital-driven ME responses and offers a systematic route to incorporate hybridization effects beyond simple low-energy models.

</details>

Method: 理论结合实验：通过平均场理论计算预测BCTL中谐波与非谐波螺旋磁态的竞争；在四方磁性Weyl半金属GdAlSi中施加外磁场，利用共振弹性X射线散射技术探测磁结构，并与理论结果进行对比验证。

Abstract: The triangular lattice antiferromagnet (TLAF) with nearest-neighbor exchange interaction is a model platform in the field of frustrated magnetism. We show that a body-centered tetragonal lattice (BCTL) can realize a similar frustration of harmonic and anharmonic helimagnetic states, and that the tetragonal magnetic Weyl semimetal GdAlSi realizes this scenario. In an applied magnetic field, resonant elastic X-ray scattering reveals a competition of harmonic cycloidal and solitonic double-Q states, well consistent with mean-field calculations. Our work provides a new paradigm for frustration physics in BCTL materials.

Abstract: CrRhAs was theoretically proposed to be a kagome metal with unusual magnetic ground states; however, little is known about its magnetic structure and physical properties experimentally. Here, we present an experimental investigation of CrRhAs with ZrNiAl-type structure and a distorted Cr kagome lattice. CrRhAs is an antiferromagnet with TN = 149 K. Powder neutron diffraction analysis reveals a noncollinear antiferromagnetic structure with propagation vector k = (1/3, 1/3, 1/2), which features a ferromagnetic second nearest neighbor coupling in the kagome plane that is different from the prediction in previous density functional theory calculations. Furthermore, CrRhAs exhibits anomalous electrical transport properties which are possibly related to multiband effects and strong spin fluctuations. For the temperature-dependent longitudinal resistivity \r{ho}xx, it is semiconductinglike above TN and becomes metallic below TN . The Hall coefficients exhibit two sign changes near 70 and 300 K. Combined with the results of heat capacity measurements, a large Kadowaki-Woods ratio α = 33.9 μΩ cm mol2 K2/J2 is obtained. The above results suggest CrRhAs is a strongly correlated kagome metal with multiband and noncollinear magnetic structure features.

Abstract: We report numerical ground states for the dipolar XY spin model, which describes extended antiferromagnetic interactions in two-dimensional arrays of polar molecules and two-level Rydberg atoms. Carrying out large-scale density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations, we compute ground state properties on nine of the eleven Archimedean lattices--tilings of the plane by regular polygons. Four of these host trivial paramagnets, while another four develop collinear Neel magnetic order, as was found previously for the square lattice. For the ordered states, we calculate the hydrodynamic parameters (magnetization, susceptibility, and stiffness) and compare to linear spin wave theory. We also investigate the triangular lattice, for which we find several competing phases including coplanar magnetism, stripe density wave order, and a possible spin liquid; their relative stability is sensitive to the long-range couplings present in our dipolar model. Finally, the Archimedean classification is completed by the kagome lattice, which we argue in a companion work is likely to be a Dirac spin liquid.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Emery模型是铜氧化物超导体的经典模型，但原始模型仅考虑最近邻氧-铜跳迁，后续研究中氧-氧跳迁的重要性也被指出。然而，对于定量准确的相图和d波序参量，常规三个跳迁参数是否足够尚不清楚，这促使本文进行系统研究。

Method: 采用动力学顶点近似（Dynamical Vertex Approximation, DVA）方法，系统性地考虑了Emery模型中不同范围的跳迁参数，包括常规的三个跳迁（最近邻氧-铜跳迁、最近邻和次近邻氧-氧跳迁）以及更远距离的跳迁，对比研究它们对超导性质的影响。

Result: 研究发现，仅使用常规三个跳迁参数可以得到与铜氧化物一致的超导穹顶，但要获得定量正确的相图和适当的d波序参量，必须包含超越这三个常规参数的长程跳迁项。

Conclusion: Emery模型用于描述铜氧化物超导体时，常规的三跳迁参数虽能定性地产生超导穹顶，但为实现定量准确的相图和正确的d波序参量，必须考虑更远距离的跳迁（即长程跳迁）。

Abstract: The Emery model is the quintessential model for cuprate superconductors. In his eponymous paper, Emery only considered the next-nearest-neighbor oxygen-copper hopping. However, long-range hoppings beyond the three conventional hopping parameters are necessary for the quantitatively correct phase diagram and for a proper d-wave order parameter.

</details>

Abstract: While deep learning offers powerful capabilities for scientific research, its application is often hindered by a lack of quantitative reliability. To address this, we introduce a probabilistic denoising framework that simultaneously extracts denoised signals and element-wise predictive uncertainties from noisy data. We demonstrate this approach on three-dimensional angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy data, showing that the model reliably recovers the spectral features of a cuprate superconductor from Poisson-distributed noise with an average count of only 0.02 electrons per voxel. Crucially, we show that these predicted uncertainties can be propagated into subsequent superconducting gap analyses, enabling quantitative parameter extraction with scientifically meaningful error bars. Furthermore, we validate the broad applicability of our approach by successfully extending it to two-dimensional X-ray diffraction data. Ultimately, this approach establishes uncertainty-aware deep learning not merely as a visualization tool, but as a rigorous framework for scientific data analysis.

</details>

*Tariq Leinen,Ola K. Forslund,Eugenio Paris,Nicola Colonna,Marco Caputo,Johan Chang,Gabriel Nagamine,Takashi Tokushima,Conny Såthe,Pascal Puphal,Jeremie Teyssier,Thorsten Schmitt,Nikolay A. Bogdanov,Maria Daghofer,Adrian L. Cavalieri,Flavio Giorgianni*

TL;DR: 本文通过Cu L3边共振非弹性X射线散射(RIXS)、宽带光学光谱和电子结构计算，系统测定了SrCu2(BO3)2 (SCBO)中局域d-d激发（1.8-2.4 eV）和电荷转移激发（吸收阈值1.2-1.6 eV，宽带~4.5 eV）的能量尺度，为这一Shastry-Sutherland模型材料的超交换磁模型提供了定量基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCBO作为Shastry-Sutherland模型的典型实现，其磁性已被广泛研究，但决定晶体场环境和Cu-O杂化的高能电子激发尚未被充分探索。

Method: 结合Cu L3边RIXS、宽带光学光谱和电子结构计算（多参考量子化学计算及DFT+U计算），系统测定SCBO中局域和带间激发能量尺度。

Result: RIXS解析出1.8-2.4 eV范围内的局域Cu2+ d-d激发，其能量和偏振依赖性与多参考量子化学计算吻合；光学光谱识别出吸收阈值约1.2-1.6 eV的电荷转移激发和~4.5 eV的高能结构，被DFT+U定性捕捉。

Conclusion: 这些结果定义了d-d和CT激发的特征能量尺度，为计算框架提供了定量基准，并为改进这一典型阻挫量子反铁磁体的超交换磁模型提供了关键输入。

Abstract: SrCu2(BO3)2 (SCBO) is a paradigmatic realization of the Shastry-Sutherland model, hosting geometrically frustrated spin dimers and a variety of quantum magnetic phases and phenomena. Although its magnetic properties have been extensively studied, the high-energy electronic excitations that determine the crystal-field environment and Cu-O hybridization have remained largely unexplored. Here we combine Cu L3-edge resonant inelastic x-ray scattering (RIXS), broadband optical spectroscopy, and electronic-structure calculations to determine the relevant local and interband excitation energy scales in SCBO. RIXS resolves a well-defined manifold of localized Cu2+ d-d excitations between 1.8 and 2.4 eV, whose energies and polarization dependence are well reproduced by multireference quantum-chemistry calculations. In contrast, optical spectroscopy identifies charge-transfer excitations with an absorption onset near 1.2-1.6 eV and a broader higher-energy structure around 4.5 eV, which are qualitatively captured by DFT+U calculations. Taken together, these results define the characteristic energy scales of d-d and CT excitations, offering quantitative benchmarks for computational frameworks and providing essential input for refining superexchange-based magnetic models of this prototypical frustrated quantum antiferromagnet.

</details>


### [81] [Shuttling of $\mathbb{Z}_4$ parafermions in an electronic ladder model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07887)

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用密度矩阵重正化群和时间相关变分原理，研究了Z4 parafermion边缘态的实时动力学及其绝热传输速度极限，为拓扑量子计算中的编织操作提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parafermion作为非阿贝尔任意子，相比Majorana费米子能实现更丰富的拓扑保护量子门，但其稳定性依赖强电子相互作用，实验和理论研究仍具挑战。实现几何编织操作需要受控的parafermion模式穿梭，本文旨在探究这一基本过程的实时动力学。

Method: 结合密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）和时间相关变分原理（TDVP）方法，研究Z4 parafermion边缘态在实时演化中的传输动力学，并评估实验相关条件下的绝热速度极限。

Result: 成功分析了Z4 parafermion边缘态的基本穿梭过程，并确定了在实验可行条件下的绝热速度极限。

Conclusion: 该研究为parafermion模式的可控传输提供了动力学理解，推进了基于非阿贝尔统计的拓扑量子计算的实际实现。

Abstract: Parafermions with non-Abelian statistics have been proposed as a promising platform for quantum computation, potentially enabling a broader set of topologically protected gates than Majorana fermions. The experimental and theoretical exploration of these exotic quasiparticles remains challenging, as their stability is linked to strong electron-electron interactions. A key step toward practical applications is the controlled shuttling of parafermionic modes, which is required for implementing geometric braiding operations. In the present work, we investigate the real-time dynamics of the elementary shuttling process by applying a combination of the density matrix renormalization group and the time-dependent variational principle approaches. We analyze the transport of $\mathbb{Z}_4$ parafermion edge states and assess the corresponding adiabatic speed limit under experimentally relevant conditions.

</details>


### [82] [Microscopic Magnetism of A(TiO)Cu4(PO4)4 (A = Ba, Pb, Sr): 31P and 63,65Cu NMR Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07946)
*Riho Rästa,Ivo Heinmaa,Joosep Link,Yusuke Kousaka,Tsuyoshi Kimura,Yoshihiko Ihara,Kenta Kimura,Raivo Stern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过核磁共振（NMR）研究了手性四方杯状反铁磁体Pb(TiO)Cu₄(PO₄)₄，并与Ba/Sr同系物对比。发现31P谱在有序态分裂为三条线（而非BaTCPO的四条），内场指数β≈0.23符合准二维临界性。零场63,65Cu NMR给出内场14.50 T和四极频率32.72 MHz，计算得到Cu位空穴占据数nd=0.20(4)。PbTCPO的有序态31P内场（69.5 mT）远高于BaTCPO（35.6 mT）和SrTCPO（34.6 mT），该增强无法仅用偶极项解释，反映了转移超精细耦合和堆叠依赖的抵消效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对手性四方杯状反铁磁体Pb(TiO)Cu₄(PO₄)₄（PbTCPO）进行微观尺度的NMR研究，并与同族Ba/Sr类似物对比，以理解A位阳离子替换对超精细耦合、局域电子结构和有序态内场的影响。

Method: 采用31P和63,65Cu核磁共振（NMR）技术，包括频率扫描谱、晶体旋转NMR和零场NMR测量，结合点电荷电场梯度计算和Sternheimer修正，提取超精细耦合常数、内场、四极频率及Cu位空穴占据数，并与Ba/Sr类似物对比。

Result: （1）顺磁态31P Knight位移追踪体磁化率，得到近各向同性转移超精细耦合Hhf[010]=6.77(3)和Hhf[001]=6.19(3) kOe/μB；（2）有序态31P谱分裂为三条线（而非BaTCPO的四条），内场序参量指数β≈0.23；（3）零场63,65Cu NMR得到Bint=14.50 T，νQ=32.72 MHz，Cu位nd=0.20(4)；（4）PbTCPO有序态31P内场69.5 mT，远高于BaTCPO（35.6 mT）和SrTCPO（34.6 mT）。

Conclusion: PbTCPO的31P内场增强源于转移超精细贡献和堆叠依赖的抵消效应的共同作用，而Hhf随A位阳离子变化反映了Cu-O-P共价性的改变。该工作揭示了A位替换对四方杯状反铁磁家族超精细耦合和磁结构的重要影响。

Abstract: We report a comprehensive NMR study of the chiral square-cupola antiferromagnet Pb(TiO)Cu$_4$(PO$_4$)$_4$ and compare its microscopic hyperfine and local-field parameters with the Ba/Sr analogues in the $A$(TiO)Cu$_4$(PO$_4$)$_4$ family. Above $T_{\rm N}\simeq 6.7$ K, the $^{31}$P Knight shift tracks the bulk susceptibility and yields nearly isotropic transferred hyperfine couplings $H_{\rm hf}^{[010]}=6.77(3)$ and $H_{\rm hf}^{[001]}=6.19(3)$ kOe/$μ_{\rm B}$. Below $T_{\rm N}$, the frequency-swept $^{31}$P spectrum splits into three lines, in contrast to the four-line pattern reported for BaTCPO. The line separation tracks the onset of the static $^{31}$P internal field with a power-law exponent $β\simeq 0.23$, consistent with quasi-two-dimensional criticality. Crystal-rotation $^{31}$P NMR in the ordered state resolves all eight symmetry-related P sites and their site-dependent anisotropy. In the ordered state, zero-field $^{63,65}$Cu NMR gives a Cu-site internal field $B_{\rm int}=14.50(6)$ T and a quadrupole frequency $ν_Q=32.72(5)$ MHz, while point-charge electric-field-gradient calculations including Sternheimer corrections yield an on-site Cu hole occupancy $n_d=0.20(4)$, consistent with a ligand-hole-dominated charge-transfer character. Comparing PbTCPO with BaTCPO and SrTCPO, we find that the transferred hyperfine coupling $H_{\rm hf}$ varies across the series, reflecting changes in local Cu-O-P covalency, whereas the ordered-state $^{31}$P internal field in PbTCPO is $69.5$ mT, considerably higher than in BaTCPO ($35.6$ mT) and SrTCPO ($34.6$ mT). This enhancement is not captured by dipolar terms alone and points to the combined effects of transferred contributions and stacking-dependent cancellation.

</details>


### [83] [Gapped 1/9 Magnetization Plateau in the Anisotropic Kagome Antiferromagnet Y-kapellasite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08067)
*Dipranjan Chatterjee,Paul A. Goddard,Ewan R. P. Thomas,Katharina M. Zoch,Hank C. H. Wu,Benjamin M. Huddart,Cornelius Krellner,Edwin Kermarrec,Mladen Horvatić,Steffen Krämer,Pascal Puphal,John Singleton,Stephen J. Blundell,Fabrice Bert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome反铁磁体中分数磁化平台的微观起源尚未解决，尤其是理论预测但实验罕见的1/9平台的本质仍不清楚。

Method: 在S=1/2各向异性kagome反铁磁体Y-kapellasite单晶上进行脉冲磁场磁化测量和高场³⁵Cl NMR实验。

Result: 观测到1/3和1/9平台及一个低场弱特征；1/9平台伴随有序局域自旋构型、低能自旋涨落被强烈抑制及激活行为，符合有能隙分数态特征。

Conclusion: 1/9分数平台对应一个有能隙的分数态，且该态对不同材料中的磁性细节具有出乎意料的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fractional magnetization plateaus provide a sensitive probe of many-body spin states in frustrated quantum magnets, yet their microscopic origin in kagome antiferromagnets remains unresolved. This is particularly true of the mysterious $1/9$ plateau, which is predicted by theory but infrequently observed in experiment. Here, we investigate this problem in the $S = 1/2$ anisotropic kagome antiferromagnet Y-kapellasite, Y$_3$Cu$_9$(OH)$_{19}$Cl$_8$, using pulsed-field magnetization measurements on single crystals and high-field $^{35}$Cl NMR. We identify a hierarchy of field-induced fractional features, including $1/3$ and $1/9$ plateaus, as well as a weaker low-field feature. Analysis of the NMR spectra and the magnetic susceptibility across the $1/9$ plateau demonstrate that it is accompanied by an ordered local spin configuration, a strong suppression of low-energy spin fluctuations and activated behavior, consistent with a gapped fractional state. These features differ from those in the only other material YCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Br$_2$[Br$_{1-y}$(OH)$_y$] in which this plateau is observed, implying a surprising robustness of the $1/9$ state to the details of the underlying magnetism.

</details>

### [84] [Quo nomine vis vocari? A random-copying model explains the temporal sequence of papal names](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07028)
*Egor Lappo,Noah A. Rosenberg*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文利用教皇名字选择这一跨越2000年的人类文化过程，研究发现尽管每个教皇的名字都是经过深思熟虑的个人决定，但从长期序列来看，教皇名字的选择符合群体遗传学中的Ewens抽样理论和中国餐馆过程（随机复制现有名字，频率越高越容易被复制，并可能创新新名字）。这表明长期的人类文化聚合行为可能服从简单的统计规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索文化演化过程中人类行为变体如何被选择的机制，利用教皇名字选择作为长期文化过程的独特案例，检验简单统计模型是否能描述看似复杂的人类文化行为。

Method: 使用Ewens抽样理论和中国餐馆过程（随机复制模型）分析教皇名字的长期选择序列，将教皇名字选择建模为以频率比例随机复制已有名字并允许创新（突变）的过程。

Result: 教皇名字的长期序列与随机复制模型的预测一致，尽管个体教皇的名字选择是精心考虑的个人决定，但聚合的文化行为在2000年的时间尺度上可用简单统计规律描述。

Conclusion: 长期的人类文化聚合行为可能遵循简单的统计规律（如随机复制模型），但特定历史事件可能导致暂时偏离该模型。

Abstract: The study of cultural evolution seeks to understand the processes by which behavioral variants are chosen in cultures over time, often as the result of large numbers of individual human choices. The selection of new popes, each of whom chooses a papal name -- typically reusing previous names in reference to previous popes -- is among the longest ongoing cultural processes taking place in a single human institution. Here, we use the record of papal names as a setting for long-term analysis of human cultural behavior. Although papal name choices are careful individual decisions, we find that the long-term sequence of papal names accords with predictions of a family of models developed in population genetics and stochastic processes -- Ewens sampling theory and the Chinese restaurant process -- which in the case of papal names amounts to randomly copying an existing name in proportion to its frequency, with the possibility of innovation of new names (mutations). Hence, despite the consideration that enters into choices of individual papal names, aggregate cultural behavior in a 2000-year old human process can potentially be described with simple laws. We discuss instances in which particular historical events might have caused temporary deviations from the random-copying model.

</details>


### [85] [Modeling the Impact of Exposed Cases in a Hantavirus Outbreak on a Cruise Ship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07498)
*Jiaming Cui*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究构建了离散时间随机SEIRD模型，结合集合调整卡尔曼滤波，分析游轮上汉坦病毒变异株的传播动力学。估计基本再生数R₀=2.76，表明严格隔离前存在显著持续传播潜力。模型揭示早期存在大量未被识别的暴露感染者，形成症状监测难以发现的隐性传染源，强调了在密闭旅行环境中加强快速监测、广泛检测和针对性隔离的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游轮上出现汉坦病毒变异株构成重大公共卫生威胁，需量化其传播动力学特征，评估潜在暴露感染者规模及暴发风险，为防控策略提供依据。

Method: 采用离散时间随机SEIRD（易感-暴露-感染-康复-死亡）模型，结合集合调整卡尔曼滤波（EAKF）进行参数推断和潜在疾病状态估计，利用WHO和ECDC疫情报告病例数据校准模型。

Result: 基本再生数R₀=2.76（95% CI: 2.52-2.99），表明严格隔离前存在显著持续传播能力；模拟显示早期存在大量未识别的暴露感染者，构成症状监测难以检测的隐性传染源。

Conclusion: 在游轮等密闭聚集环境中，需加强快速监测、广泛检测、针对性隔离和暴露个体的主动监控；该建模框架可为类似密集和空间受限人群中的传染病暴发评估和干预规划提供支持。

Abstract: The emergence of a hantavirus variant aboard a commercial cruise ship presents a significant public health concern. This study develops a discrete-time stochastic Susceptible-Exposed-Infectious-Recovered-Dead model to estimate transmission dynamics, hidden exposed infections, and outbreak risk among passengers and crew. Epidemiological parameters and latent disease states were inferred using an Ensemble Adjustment Kalman Filter calibrated to reported case data from WHO and ECDC situation reports. The estimated basic reproduction number was 2.76, with a 95\% confidence interval of 2.52-2.99, indicating substantial potential for sustained onboard transmission before strict quarantine measures. Simulations further suggest that several exposed individuals may remain unidentified during the early outbreak phase, creating a hidden reservoir that symptom-based surveillance alone may fail to detect. These findings highlight the importance of rapid surveillance, widespread testing, targeted quarantine, and active monitoring of exposed individuals in confined travel settings. The proposed modeling framework can support timely outbreak assessment and intervention planning for infectious-disease events in similarly dense and spatially constrained populations.

</details>

### [86] [The study of the circumnuclear environment of accreting supermassive black holes with realistic X-ray spectral models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06794)
*Georgios Dimopoulos,Claudio Ricci,Stéphane Paltani*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用射线追踪代码RefleX，针对活动星系核（AGN）周围物质构型简单化的问题，开发了两种新的X射线谱模型（RXToPo和RXagn1），其中RXagn1模型首次同时包含了尘埃环、极向空心锥、吸积盘和宽线区等现实几何结构。将这两个模型应用于NGC 424的X射线谱，展示了其在研究以再处理辐射为主的X射线源方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的X射线谱模型虽然能很好拟合数据，但缺乏现实几何构型，大多采用简化结构（如平板或环面），需要开发更具真实几何特征的模型来更准确地研究活动星系核的核周物质性质。

Method: 使用射线追踪代码RefleX，构建包含尘埃环和极向空心锥的RXToPo模型，以及额外包含吸积盘和宽线区的RXagn1模型，生成覆盖宽能量范围的X射线谱表模型。

Result: 成功开发了两个公开可用的新表模型RXToPo和RXagn1，并将其应用于NGC 424的X射线谱分析，验证了模型在研究以再处理辐射为主的源方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 新模型采用更现实的几何构型，为研究AGN中再处理辐射主导的X射线发射提供了更有力的工具，弥补了现有模型几何简化的不足。

Abstract: X-ray spectral modeling is a powerful tool for studying the immediate environment of accreting objects, including supermassive black holes. Several models, either phenomenological or physically driven, have been developed over the past decade to study X-ray spectra, delivering important insights into the properties of circumnuclear material of active galactic nuclei (AGN). Despite the fact that these models are able to reproduce the data well, they often lack realistic geometries, and most of them consist of simplified configurations such as a slab or a torus. We use the ray-tracing code \textsc{RefleX} to generate new spectral models that cover a wide energy range in the X-ray band, adopting a realistic configuration for the surrounding material. We introduce two new table models that are publicly available: 1) the RXToPo model, which features an X-ray source along with a dusty torus and a polar hollow cone; 2) the RXagn1 model, which includes, besides the torus and polar cone, also the accretion disk and the broad line region. Both models were applied to the X-ray spectrum of NGC 424, demonstrating their potential to study sources whose X-ray emission is dominated by reprocessed radiation.

</details>


### [87] [X-ray spectroscopy mass constraints on V1674 Her: the fastest nova does not have a near-Chandrasekhar white dwarf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06917)
*Tekeba Olbemo,Manel Errando,Andrea Gokus*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对V1674 Her（2021年赫拉克勒斯新星）的宽带X射线研究，直接约束了白矮星质量（约1.09-1.12 M☉）和磁场强度（约21.3 MG），发现其质量远低于从经验衰减时间关系推断的Chandrasekhar极限值，表明极快新星不一定需要近Chandrasekhar质量的白矮星，新星时标还受其他参数影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V1674 Her是光学衰减时间最快（t₂~1天）的经典新星，传统解释认为这暗示白矮星质量接近Chandrasekhar极限。本研究旨在通过直接X射线观测约束其白矮星质量和磁场强度，检验经验衰减时间关系的可靠性。

Method: 联合使用XMM-Newton和NuSTAR进行宽带X射线观测；采用物理驱动的激波后吸积柱模型（考虑温度梯度和白矮星表面反射）对硬X射线发射建模；通过X射线功率谱时域分析独立约束白矮星质量；基于吸积率和磁球半径估算表面磁场强度。

Result: 吸积柱模型给出白矮星质量M=1.09^{+0.07}_{-0.06} M☉，时域分析给出M=1.12±0.06 M☉，均显著低于从衰减时间关系推断的值；表面磁场强度B=21.3^{+6.6}_{-5.7}(stat)^{+12.9}_{-8.1}(sys) MG，处于中间极化星磁场分布的高端。

Conclusion: 最快的经典新星并不一定拥有近Chandrasekhar极限质量的白矮星，经验衰减时间关系可能高估极端快新星的白矮星质量；白矮星质量以外的其他参数（如磁场、吸积率）在新星时标设定中起关键作用；直接X射线约束对于准确测量白矮星参数至关重要。

Abstract: V1674 Her (Nova Her 2021) is the fastest classical nova ever recorded, with an optical decline time of $t_2 \sim 1$ day, typically interpreted as evidence for a white dwarf mass close to the Chandrasekhar limit. We present a broadband X-ray study of V1674 Her combining contemporaneous XMM-Newton and NuSTAR observations in quiescence to directly constrain the white dwarf mass and magnetic field strength. The hard X-ray emission is modeled using a physically motivated post-shock accretion column model that accounts for the temperature gradient in the flow and reflection from the white dwarf surface. Under the assumption that the accretion disk is truncated at the co-rotation radius, we obtain a white dwarf mass of $M = 1.09^{+0.07}_{-0.06}\,M_\odot$. An independent constraint derived from timing analysis of the X-ray power spectrum yields a consistent value of $M = 1.12 \pm 0.06\,M_\odot$. These values are significantly lower than those inferred from empirical decline-time relations, suggesting that such relations may overestimate white dwarf masses in extreme fast novae. From the inferred accretion rate and magnetospheric radius, we estimate a surface magnetic field strength of $B = 21.3^{+6.6}_{-5.7}\,(\mathrm{stat})^{+12.9}_{-8.1}\,(\mathrm{sys})\,\mathrm{MG}$, placing V1674 Her at the high end of the magnetic field distribution for intermediate polars. Our results demonstrate that even the fastest novae do not necessarily host near-Chandrasekhar white dwarfs, highlighting the importance of direct X-ray constraints and suggesting that additional parameters beyond white dwarf mass play a key role in setting nova timescales.
