  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波观测重新激发了对黑洞质量间隙及其下缘（第一代黑洞最大质量）的兴趣。\(^{12}{\rm C}(α,γ)^{16}{\rm O}\) 反应决定了核氦燃烧后的碳氧比，进而影响大质量恒星向脉动对不稳定性和对不稳定性超新星的演化，因此对该反应 S 因子的精确约束对于理解黑洞质量间隙至关重要。然而，从引力波种群推断得出的 S(300 keV) 约束存在模型依赖性，需要与独立的核物理约束保持一致。

Method: 重新分析了低能 \(^{12}{\rm C}(α,γ)^{16}{\rm O}\) S 因子，利用亚阈值 \(1^{-}\) 和 \(2^{+}\) 态 ANC（渐近归一化常数）、\(^{16}{\rm O}\) 基态 ANC 的最新信息，并结合直接俘获数据，得到 ANC 约束下的 S(300 keV) 范围。再利用该 S 因子与对不稳定性质量间隙下缘之间的变换关系，估计了第一代黑洞质量间隙的下缘。

Result: 更新后的核约束支持比旧有中心评估更低的 S(300 keV) 值，并排除了一些黑洞种群模型所要求的极大值。基于此，估计第一代黑洞质量间隙的下缘为 \(M_{\rm BH}/M_\odot \simeq 61\text{--}75\)。

Conclusion: 当前的核物理约束倾向于一个相对较高的第一代黑洞质量间隙下缘（~61–75 \(M_\odot\)），这对于解释引力波观测到的黑洞质量分布具有重要意义，同时指出从引力波种群推断中得出的 S(300 keV) 值必须与独立的核物理约束保持一致。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave observations have renewed interest in the black-hole mass gap and in the maximum mass of first-generation black holes below its lower edge. The \(^{12}{\rm C}(α,γ)^{16}{\rm O}\) reaction plays a central role in this problem because it determines the carbon-to-oxygen ratio after core-helium burning and thereby affects the later evolution of massive stars toward pulsational pair instability and pair-instability supernovae.
  Recent attempts to constrain \(S(300~{\rm keV})\) from gravitational-wave population inferences face important limitations, because the lower edge of the black-hole mass gap is not directly measured. It is inferred model dependently from assumptions about stellar evolution, metallicity, mass loss, rotation, binary evolution, hierarchical mergers, selection effects, priors, and the adopted population model. Therefore, values of \(S(300~{\rm keV})\) inferred from black-hole populations must remain consistent with independent nuclear-physics constraints.
  In this work we reanalyze the low-energy \(^{12}{\rm C}(α,γ)^{16}{\rm O}\) \(S\) factor using updated information on the subthreshold \(1^{-}\) and \(2^{+}\) ANCs and on the ground-state ANC of \(^{16}{\rm O}\), together with direct capture data. These constraints favor a lower \(S(300~{\rm keV})\) than the older central evaluation and disfavor very large values required by some black-hole-population interpretations. Using the resulting ANC-constrained \(S(300~{\rm keV})\) range and the transformed relation between this quantity and the lower edge of the pair-instability mass gap, we estimate \[ \frac{M_{\rm BH}}{M_\odot}\simeq 61\text{--}75 . \] Thus, the present nuclear-physics constraints favor a relatively high lower edge of the first-generation black-hole mass gap.

### [89] [Anisotropic Thermal Conduction as a Driver of Jet Collimation and Magnetic Field Amplification on Cold Fronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07235)

TL;DR: 本文通过二维MHD模拟，研究了各向异性热传导对星系团中AGN喷流形态和磁场结构的影响。结果表明，热传导将喷流头部的热量沿磁力线向后输运至内茧层，增加内茧压力，使喷流准直程度提升约4倍，磁场强度最大提升约1.5倍，且准直效果随热传导效率增加而增强，揭示了"热传导准直机制"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团内热传导如何影响等离子体动力学及其有效运行条件尚不明确。AGN喷流与冷锋相互作用的系统为研究热传导、喷流动力学与有序磁场之间的相互作用提供了独特环境，因此需要量化热传导对AGN喷流形态和周围磁场构型的影响。

Method: 采用二维磁流体动力学（MHD）模拟，在ICM环境中模拟AGN喷流，并纳入不同效率的各向异性热传导。

Result: 热传导将喷流头部的热量沿磁力线向后输运至内茧层，增加内茧压力，使喷流准直程度提升约4倍；磁场沿冷锋面拉伸，最大场强提升约1.5倍；准直效果随热传导效率增加而增强。

Conclusion: 各向异性热传导能在星系团喷流尺度上有效运作，在解释合并星系团环境中的喷流形态和磁场结构时，考虑热传导效应可能是重要的。

Abstract: Galaxy clusters contain a hot, diffuse, and weakly magnetized plasma known as the intracluster medium (ICM). In this environment, how thermal conduction influences plasma dynamics and the conditions under which it operates efficiently remain open questions in cluster physics. Systems in which active galactic nuclei (AGN) jets interact with cold fronts produced by cluster mergers provide a unique setting to examine the interplay between conduction, jet dynamics, and ordered magnetic fields. To interpret the detailed structures revealed by recent observations, it is therefore important, as a first theoretical step, to quantify how thermal conduction modifies AGN jet morphology and the surrounding magnetic-field configuration. We perform two-dimensional magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) simulations of an AGN jet in an ICM environment, incorporating anisotropic thermal conduction with varying efficiency. The simulations show that thermal conduction transports heat from the jet head backward along magnetic field lines into the inner cocoon. This process increases the inner cocoon pressure, enhancing jet collimation by a factor of $\sim 4$ compared to models without conduction. This stronger collimation stretches the magnetic fields along the cold-front surface, resulting in a maximum field strength up to a factor of $\sim 1.5$ larger. Jet collimation increases as the conduction efficiency increases, which is interpreted as a conductive collimation mechanism. These results suggest that anisotropic thermal conduction can operate effectively on jet scales in galaxy clusters, and that accounting for conduction may be important when interpreting jet morphology and magnetic field structure in merging cluster environments.

</details>


### [90] [Resonant Inverse Compton Scattering and Hard X-ray Emission in Magnetar Magnetospheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07255)
*Kun Hu,Nicholas Rackers,Alexander Y. Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于磁星磁层中的对流出流框架，计算了共振康普顿散射的光深、能谱和偏振特性。研究发现共振冷却会显著改变磁层等离子体密度，并对硬X射线辐射区域施加严格的光深约束。在IXPE观测的几何条件下，赤道扭曲构型可以解释4U 0142+61的NuSTAR硬X射线谱，而极区扭曲构型则被排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星是表面磁场极强的中子星亚类，部分磁星表现出持续硬X射线辐射（幂律谱，光子指数1-1.5，能量范围~10 keV到数百keV）。解释这一硬X射线成分的主要机制是共振康普顿散射，即热种子光子被沿磁层磁力线运动的相对论性电子-正电子对逆康普顿散射。本文旨在系统计算该过程的物理特性并约束辐射区域几何构型。

Method: 采用磁星磁层中的对流出流框架，计算共振康普顿散射的光深（opacity），以及逆康普顿散射产生的能谱和偏振特性。结合IXPE的观测几何信息，分别测试赤道扭曲和极区扭曲两种磁层构型，并与NuSTAR对4U 0142+61的硬X射线观测谱进行对比。

Result: 1) 共振冷却会显著改变磁层等离子体密度分布，并对硬X射线辐射区域施加严格的光深约束；2) 在IXPE观测几何下，赤道附近的扭曲构型能够成功再现4U 0142+61的NuSTAR硬X射线谱；3) 极区扭曲构型与该源的观测谱不一致，被排除。

Conclusion: 联合光谱、时变和偏振建模对于区分磁层散射几何构型、理解对等离子体的物理性质至关重要。赤道扭曲构型是4U 0142+61硬X射线辐射的可行方案，未来的多维度联合观测将有助于进一步约束磁星磁层物理。

Abstract: Magnetars are a subclass of neutron stars with ultra-strong surface magnetic fields. Some magnetars exhibit persistent hard X-ray emission, characterized by power-law tails with photon indices around 1--1.5, extending from ${\sim}$10 keV to several hundred keV. The leading explanation for this hard X-ray component is resonant Compton scattering, in which the thermal seed photons are upscattered by relativistic electron-positron pairs flowing along magnetic field lines in the magnetosphere. In this work, we adopt the pair outflow framework of the magnetar magnetosphere and calculate the resonant Compton scattering opacity, as well as the spectrum and polarization of the upscattered emission. We find that resonant cooling can substantially modify the magnetospheric plasma density and impose strong optical depth constraints on the hard X-ray emission regions. Under the viewing geometry inferred from IXPE, an equatorial twist near the stellar surface provides a viable configuration for the NuSTAR hard X-ray spectrum of 4U 0142+61, while a polar-twist geometry is disfavored. Joint spectral, timing, and polarimetric modeling will be essential for distinguishing between the magnetospheric scattering geometries and understanding the physical properties of the pair plasma.

Result: 发现显著延展GeV辐射（半径1.02度，显著性10.6σ）；伽马射线能谱光子指数2.88；周围H2和HII气体质量约289太阳质量；推导出宇宙线加速效率与扩散系数之间的关系。

Abstract: We report a detailed analysis on the young stellar cluster Berkeley 59 using Fermi-LAT. Using up-to-date source catalog and background models, we found significant extended GeV emission around Berkeley 59, which can be modeled by a radial disk of 1.02 degree radius with a significance of the extension of 10.6 sigma. We investigated the molecular, neutral and ionized gas content and the hadronic origin. The gamma-ray spectrum of Berkeley 59 has a photon index of 2.88. The derived gas mass from H2 and HII around Berkeley 59 is about 289 solar mass. We derived the relationship between cosmic ray acceleration efficiency and diffusion coefficient. Our results suggest that the extended gamma-ray emission originates from cosmic rays accelerated by cluster winds interacting with surrounding gas.

Conclusion: KM3-230213A事件开启了超高能中微子天文学的新时代，但其与现有上限的矛盾表明要么现有上限存在系统误差，要么该事件来自特殊源；需要对源群体模型进行更严格的约束。

Abstract: The recent detection of the ultra-high energy neutrino KM3-230213A by KM3NeT/ARCA marks the first observation of an astrophysical neutrino with energy above 100 PeV, opening a new window to the ultra-high energy Universe. In this contribution, the current global ultra-high energy neutrino landscape in light of this event is reviewed, including tension of this observation with existing limits set by the IceCube and the Pierre Auger Observatories. Different scenarios are discussed to explain its origin. Recent efforts to constrain features of potential source populations using the inferred diffuse ultra-high energy neutrino flux are also presented.

</details>


### [93] [Advance warning of $γ$-ray blazar flares from \textit{Fermi}-LAT light curves: a strictly causal machine-learning backtest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07680)
*Zahir Shah,Sikandar Akbar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种严格因果的机器学习框架，利用Fermi-LAT的3天分箱光变曲线预测耀变体伽马射线耀斑。通过贝叶斯区段识别耀斑区间，从365天滑动窗口中提取42个变异性特征，分别训练WATCH（预测90天内是否出现耀斑活动）和TRIGGER（预测45天内是否出现新耀斑爆发）模型。使用多项式逻辑回归在FSRQ 4FGL J1048.4+7143上取得最佳表现：WATCH ROC AUC=0.891，平均精度=0.396，召回率0.86；TRIGGER AUC=0.770。WATCH在两次持出耀斑事件前均发出警报，分别提前4.5天和2.5天，对应的活跃期分别提前88.5天和72.5天开始。结果表明长期Fermi光变曲线包含可用于预测耀斑爆发的有用信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用长期Fermi-LAT监测数据，探究在耀斑变得明显于伽马射线发射之前，耀变体光变曲线是否显示出即将发生耀斑的迹象，从而实现对伽马射线耀斑的提前预报。

Method: 提出严格因果的机器学习框架：使用贝叶斯区段识别耀斑区间；从365天滑动窗口中提取42个变异性特征；分别训练WATCH（90天预警）和TRIGGER（45天预警）模型；所有标定、校准、阈值选择和验证仅使用MJD 60000之前的截断数据以避免时间泄漏；比较逻辑回归、多项式逻辑回归和随机森林分类器。

Result: 多项式逻辑回归在持出测试集上表现最佳：WATCH ROC AUC=0.891，平均精度=0.396，块置换概率p_perm=0.006，召回率0.86（21个正窗口中的18个）；TRIGGER AUC=0.770，TRIGGER AP=0.123。WATCH在两次持出耀斑事件前分别提前4.5天和2.5天发出警报，活跃期分别提前88.5天和72.5天开始。

Conclusion: 长期Fermi光变曲线包含有用的预测信息，能够指示耀变体耀斑的构建过程，可实现一定程度的提前预警。

Abstract: Long-term \textit{Fermi}-LAT monitoring makes it possible to ask whether a blazar light curve shows signs of an upcoming flare before the flare becomes obvious in the $γ$-ray emission. We present a strictly causal machine-learning framework for forecasting $γ$-ray blazar flares from 3-d binned LAT light curves. Flare intervals are identified with Bayesian Blocks, and each light curve is sampled with 365-d trailing windows from which 42 variability features are measured. We train separate WATCH and TRIGGER models: WATCH predicts whether flare activity will appear within the next 90 d, while TRIGGER predicts whether a new flare onset will occur within the next 45 d. To avoid temporal leakage, all scaling, calibration, threshold selection, and validation use only the pre-cutoff data before MJD 60000. We apply the method to the FSRQ 4FGL\,J1048.4$+$7143, using 13 bright blazars as auxiliary training sources. Among logistic regression, polynomial logistic regression, and random forest classifiers, polynomial logistic regression gives the strongest held-out WATCH performance, with ROC AUC $=0.891$, average precision $=0.396$, and a block-permutation probability $p_{\rm perm}=0.006$. At the selected WATCH threshold, it recovers 18 of the 21 positive windows in the held-out WATCH set, corresponding to a recall of 0.86. The same model also gives the best held-out TRIGGER ranking, with TRIGGER AUC $=0.770$ and TRIGGER AP $=0.123$, although no reliable pre-onset TRIGGER alert is obtained. The WATCH state appears before both held-out flare episodes, with final alerts 4.5 and 2.5 d before onset. The corresponding broader WATCH-active periods begin 88.5 and 72.5 d before flare onset. These results suggest that long-term {\fermi} light curves contain useful predictive information about the build-up to blazar flares.

### [94] [Young Massive Star Clusters as TeV Emitters: Constraints from H.E.S.S. and LHAASO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07777)
*Rowan Batzofin,Pierre Cristofari,Kathrin Egberts*

Abstract: Young massive star clusters (YMSCs) have been proposed as excellent candidates for the main sources of Galactic cosmic rays (CRs) up to the PeV range. The detection and study of gamma rays in the very-high-energy (E>100GeV) range has brought arguments in favour of this hypothesis. Current instruments have detected only a few YMSCs. Future observatories are expected to increase this number, providing a larger sample improving our ability to constrain the role of YMSCs in the origin of CRs. We study the population of TeV YMSCs detected and their properties, confronting simulations of the YMSC population to the observed sample, to address the fundamental questions concerning the spectrum of accelerated particles, the efficiency of CR production, and the fraction of the wind luminosity converted into turbulent magnetic fields. Using Monte Carlo methods, we simulate the Galactic population of YMSCs in the gamma-ray domain and confront our simulations to the catalogue of sources of the systematic survey of the Galactic plane performed by H.E.S.S. (HGPS) and the First LHAASO Catalogue of Gamma-Ray Sources. We systematically explore the parameter space of our model, including the slope of accelerated particles $α$, the CR efficiency $η_{\rm CR}$, the fraction of the wind luminosity converted into turbulent magnetic field $η_{\rm b}$, and the diffusion regime. We found 5 possible sets of parameters for which >75% of realisations agree with the combined data from the HGPS and LHAASO 1st catalogue. Certain regions of the parameter space are strongly disfavoured, such as Bohm diffusion. Our model successfully reproduces the YMSC population observed in both catalogues. With future systematic surveys, e.g. the Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO), this approach will help break degeneracies and improve our understanding of particle acceleration at YMSC shocks in the Galaxy.

</details>


### [95] [Detection of persistent helium absorption in the 91bg-like type Ia Supernova 2022an](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07827)
*Ping Chen,Avishay Gal-Yam,Subo Dong,Renyue Cen,Boaz Katz,Kate Maguire,Steve Schulze,Jesper Sollerman,Joseph P Anderson,Ting-Wan Chen,L. Galbany,Mariusz Gromadzki,Chang Liu,Adam A. Miller,Tomás E. Müller-Bravo,Tanja Petrushevska,Giuliano Pignata*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 论文报告了快速衰减型Ia超新星SN 2022an的光学和近红外观测，发现其光谱中在约1.037μm处存在持久的He I 1.083μm吸收特征，这是迄今为止在91bg-like Ia超新星中发现的最有力氦抛射物证据，支持亚钱德拉塞卡质量双爆燃模型，并揭示了近红外光谱在诊断热核超新星爆炸中的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 91bg-like Ia超新星中是否存在氦抛射物一直存在争议，此前缺乏直接观测证据。现有理论预测亚钱德拉塞卡质量双爆燃模型会产生表面氦层抛射物，但缺乏与观测对应的光谱计算结果。

Method: 对SN 2022an进行光学和近红外测光与光谱观测，分析其光度和光谱特征（特别是1.037μm附近的窄吸收特征），测量其半高全宽、蓝移速度等参数，并与多个光学He I跃迁进行比较验证。

Result: SN 2022an被确认为标准91bg-like事件，其近红外光谱在1.037μm处检测到He I 1.083μm吸收线，FWHM为75Å，蓝移速度1.3×10⁴ km/s，FWHM速度2.1×10³ km/s，该特征在爆发后30至90天内持续存在；早期还检测到更高速度（~1.5×10⁴ km/s）的光学He I跃迁。

Conclusion: SN 2022an中的氦吸收源自外层抛射物中的未燃尽物质，而非伴星剥离的氦，为91bg-like Ia超新星中存在氦抛射物提供了迄今最强证据，支持亚钱德拉塞卡质量双爆燃模型；当前缺乏与观测对应的理论计算，近红外光谱是诊断热核超新星爆炸的有效工具。

Abstract: We present optical and near-infrared observations of the fast-declining Type Ia supernova (SN Ia) 2022an. The photometric and spectroscopic properties identify it as a standard 91bg-like event; however, our data reveal a relatively narrow absorption feature with a full width at half maximum (FWHM) of 75 angstroms near $1.037\,μ$m in the rest frame of the observed spectra that persists from around 30 days to nearly 90 days after maximum light. We attribute this feature to He I $1.083\,μ$m line with a blueshifted velocity of $1.3\times10^{4}$ km s$^{-1}$ and a FWHM of $2.1\times10^{3}$ km s$^{-1}$, supported by the detection of multiple optical He I transitions in earlier epochs at a higher velocity around $1.5\times10^{4}$ km s$^{-1}$. The high velocity of the helium could not be explained by helium external to the progenitor at the explosion, such as the stripped surface helium from a companion star. The properties of the helium absorption in SN 2022an spectra instead point to unburnt material in the outer ejecta, thus providing the most compelling evidence to date for helium-bearing ejecta in a 91bg-like SN Ia. Such helium has been predicted for sub-Chandrasekhar-mass double-detonation explosions involving a surface helium shell. No theoretical calculations of modern helium-shell double detonation have been performed at epochs similar to those observed for SN 2022an to study the effect of helium on their spectra, revealing a gap between observations and theoretical calculations in understanding the manifestation of helium in SNe Ia. Nevertheless, the discovery of persistent helium absorption in SN 2022an demonstrates the diagnostic power of NIR spectroscopy for understanding thermonuclear supernova explosions by probing the abundance and structure of their ejecta.

</details>


### [96] [Magnetar field dynamics driven by chiral anomalies without magnetic helicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08068)
*Clara Dehman*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了手征磁效应（CME）在不同初始螺旋性构型（包括无螺旋性初始条件）下对磁星偶极场产生的普适性，发现CME能在数十年时间尺度内高效产生磁星强度偶极场，且该过程独立于初始螺旋性含量，主要由最大手征化学势决定。研究还揭示了偶极场的两种演化路径：稳定通过欧姆耗散演化，或因获得足够螺旋性而通过手征反常衰变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（Dehman & Pons 2025）已证明CME在初始磁场具有净螺旋性时可有效产生磁星级偶极场，但中子星是否携带有螺旋性磁场尚不确定。本文旨在探究CME在更广泛初始螺旋性配置（包括零螺旋性）下的适用性，以解决这一不确定性。

Method: 通过在不同初始螺旋性构型（从有螺旋到无螺旋）下进行数值模拟，研究CME的演化行为，分析偶极场产生的时间尺度、触发条件（最大手征化学势μ₅^max阈值），并追踪偶极场的稳定性及后续衰变路径。

Result: 1）CME在数十年时间尺度内高效产生磁星强度偶极场（B_dip ≳ 10¹⁴ G），且与初始螺旋性含量无关；2）触发条件为最大手征化学势μ₅^max ≳ 数个×10⁻¹¹ MeV；3）偶极场可稳定演化（随后经历欧姆耗散），或因获得足够螺旋性而变得不稳定，通过手征反常将能量转移到低螺旋模式，具体取决于初始螺旋性分布。

Conclusion: CME的适用性可拓展至更现实的磁场构型（包括非螺旋初始条件），初始螺旋性分布（而非总螺旋性）对磁星磁场演化和形成至关重要，但该量在中子星形成过程中仍缺乏约束。

Abstract: The chiral magnetic effect (CME), arising from the chiral anomaly and enabling a mutual conversion between magnetic topology and fermionic chirality, is a key mechanism in magnetar field evolution. Previous work by Dehman & Pons (2025) demonstrated that the CME can efficiently generate dipolar fields ($B_{\rm dip} \gtrsim 10^{14}~\mathrm{G}$), consistent with magnetar timing measurements, provided that the initial magnetic field carries net helicity. However, whether neutron stars are born with magnetic helicity remains uncertain. In this work, we investigate the CME across a range of initial helicity configurations, including non-helical initial conditions. We find that the CME efficiently generates magnetar-strength dipoles on timescales of decades, independently of the initial helicity content. The instability is driven by localized helical structures that induce a residual chiral asymmetry and is primarily governed by the maximum chiral chemical potential, requiring $μ_5^{\rm max} \gtrsim \mathrm{few}\times10^{-11}~\mathrm{MeV}$ for onset in the magnetar regime. Our results further show that these dipoles may either remain stable and subsequently evolve through standard Ohmic decay, or become unstable if they acquire sufficient helicity, in which case they decay through the chiral anomaly, transferring energy to less helical modes. This outcome depends sensitively on the initial helicity distribution. These findings extend the applicability of the CME to more realistic magnetic-field configurations and underscore the importance of the helicity distribution at birth, a quantity that remains poorly constrained in neutron star formation, yet is crucial for determining neutron star magnetic evolution and magnetar formation.

</details>

### [97] [On the refined local Langlands conjecture for discrete $L$-parameters of inner forms of quasi-split disconnected real reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06922)
*Tasho Kaletha,Paul Mezo*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对拟分裂连通约化实群G及其内形式，在有限群A作用保持R-pinning的条件下，为每个离散L-参数构造了不连通群G⋊A的内形式上的离散系列表示L-包，并证明了其满足内窥特征恒等式，从而证实了第一作者近期提出的不连通实约化群内形式的精化局部Langlands对应猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不连通实约化群内形式上的精化局部Langlands对应，这是该领域此前尚未解决的重要猜想。准分裂连通群的理论已较成熟，但将其推广到带有限群作用的不连通情形及其内形式，需要克服表示构造和特征恒等式验证的双重困难。

Method: 利用有限群A对G的R-pinning保持自同构，对每个离散L-参数系统构造内形式G̃_z(R)上不可约离散系列表示的L-包；通过归一化转移因子验证这些L-包满足内窥特征恒等式，从而建立从L-参数到L-包的双射（满足内窥转移相容性）。

Result: 成功构造了满足内窥特征恒等式的离散系列L-包，证明了该构造与内窥转移理论相容，从而完成了对第一作者近期提出的精化局部Langlands对应猜想的证明。

Conclusion: 本文是局部Langlands纲领在不连通实约化群内形式方向的重要进展，首次建立了该情形下精化对应的完整框架，为后续研究更一般群上的Langlands对应提供了关键工具和范例。

Abstract: Given a quasi-split connected reductive $\mathbb{R}$-group $G$ and a finite group $A$ acting on $G$ by $\mathbb{R}$-automorphisms that preserve an $\mathbb{R}$-pinning, we construct for each discrete $L$-parameter for $G$ a corresponding $L$-packet of irreducible discrete series representations on each inner forms $\tilde G_z(\mathbb{R})$ of the disconnected group $\tilde G = G \rtimes A$. We prove that these $L$-packets satisfy the endoscopic character identities with respect to normalized transfer factors. This proves the conjectural refined local Langlands correspondence for inner forms of quasi-split disconnected real reductive groups, as recently formulated by the first author.

</details>


### [98] [Limits and colimits in silting theory with applications to the wall and chamber structure of an algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07555)
*Rosanna Laking,Alexandra Zvonareva*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文考虑由倾斜对象给出的嵌套t-结构族，通过同伦余极限构造对应于这些t-结构交集的倾斜对象，并对偶地通过同伦极限构造余倾斜对象。将该结果应用于构造与数值挠对及有限维代数实Grothendieck群的墙与室结构中极限墙相对应的两项大倾斜对象。特别地，当代数为驯服型时，结合Plamondon和Yurikusa的结果，可用此方法描述任意数值挠对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立倾斜/余倾斜对象与t-结构交集之间的系统构造方法，并将其应用于数值挠对和极限墙的刻画，特别是驯服代数情形下的完整描述。

Method: 利用同伦余极限构造对应于嵌套t-结构族（由倾斜对象给出）交集的倾斜对象，并利用同伦极限对偶地处理余倾斜情形。将构造应用于有限维代数的实Grothendieck群上的墙与室结构。

Result: 成功构造了与数值挠对和极限墙相对应的两项大倾斜对象；在驯服代数情形下，结合已有结果可完整描述任意数值挠对。

Conclusion: 本文建立的同伦（余）极限构造方法统一了倾斜对象与t-结构交集之间的关系，并为数值挠对和极限墙提供了系统的代数构造。

Abstract: In this paper we consider a family of nested t-structures given by silting objects and construct a silting object corresponding to the intersection of aisles of these t-structures as a homotopy colimit. The dual construction for the cosilting case is given as a homotopy limit. The results are applied to construct two-term large silting objects corresponding to the numerical torsion pairs and the limiting walls in the wall and chamber structure of the real Grothendieck group of a finite dimensional algebra. In particular, in case the algebra is tame we can describe any numerical torsion pair in this way by combining our results with results of Plamondon and Yurikusa.

</details>

### [99] [Combinatorial Models for Linear Homotopy Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07573)
*Atabey Kaygun*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文在特征零域k上，通过小微分范畴代数比较了链复形、半单纯向量空间、增广半单纯向量空间、半立方向量空间和树状向量空间。证明了半单纯模与增广半单纯模在Gabriel-Zisman局部化和Quillen模型范畴层面上等价于相应的链复形同伦理论。半立方符号嵌入给出了从半立方模到增广半单纯模的自然比较并诱导Quillen伴随，但由于-1阶增广同调存在障碍，这不是全半立方范畴上的Quillen等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不同代数结构（链复形、半单纯、半立方、树状等）之间的同伦理论等价关系，澄清它们在微分范畴代数框架下的比较关系。

Method: 通过小微分范畴代数作为统一框架，利用Gabriel-Zisman局部化和Quillen模型范畴理论，并引入半立方符号嵌入构造比较映射。

Result: 半单纯模与增广半单纯模等价于链复合同伦理论；半立方符号嵌入诱导Quillen伴随，但非Quillen等价，障碍在于-1阶增广同调。

Conclusion: 不同代数结构间的同伦理论等价性并非全部成立，半立方情形存在本质障碍。

Abstract: For a field $k$ of characteristic $0$, we compare $k$-linear chain complexes, semisimplicial vector spaces, augmented semisimplicial vector spaces, semicubical vector spaces, and arboreal vector spaces through small differential categorical algebras. We prove that semisimplicial modules and augmented semisimplicial modules are equivalent to appropriate chain-complex homotopy theories, both at the Gabriel--Zisman localization and the Quillen model-categorical level. The semicubical sign embedding gives a natural comparison from semicubical modules to augmented semisimplicial modules and induces a Quillen adjunction, but not a Quillen equivalence on the full semicubical category since there is an obstruction in augmented homology at degree $-1$.

</details>


### [100] [Shape theory for condensed anima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07843)
*Catrin Mair*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文从不同角度定义了凝聚泛像（condensed anima）的“形状”概念，证明了该概念在仿紧致紧生成空间和局部可缩空间的情形下能够恢复经典拓扑空间的形状概念，从而推广了Clausen-Scholze和Haine关于层和凝聚上同调的比较结果。此外，论文还研究了凝聚泛像的凝聚同伦群，并借助拟拓扑群描述了凝聚群上的底层拓扑群函子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为凝聚泛像建立合适的“形状”概念，并证明它能恢复经典拓扑空间的形状理论，从而统一和推广已有的比较结果。

Method: 通过多种视角定义凝聚泛像的形状概念，利用仿紧致紧生成空间和局部可缩空间的性质进行证明，并引入拟拓扑群来刻画凝聚群的底层拓扑结构。

Result: 证明了凝聚泛像的形状概念在仿紧致紧生成空间和局部可缩空间中与经典形状概念等价；延伸了Clausen-Scholze和Haine的比较结果；给出了凝聚群底层拓扑群函子的拟拓扑群描述。

Conclusion: 凝聚泛像的形状概念是经典形状理论的推广，并在特定条件下与之等价，同时为凝聚同伦群和凝聚群结构提供了新见解。

Abstract: We give different perspectives on the notion of shape for condensed anima. We prove that it recovers more classical notions of shape for topological spaces in the cases of all paracompact compactly generated spaces and all locally contractible spaces. These recovering statements imply and extend comparison results on sheaf and condensed cohomology by Clausen-Scholze and Haine. Another homotopy-theoretical direction for condensed anima are their condensed homotopy groups. Connected to this, we give a description of the underlying topological group functor on condensed groups via quasi-topological groups.

</details>


### [101] [Spectra of bi-incomplete Tambara functors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07895)
*Scott Balchin,J. D. Quigley,Ben Spitz*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文定义了任意双不完全Tambara函子的素理想谱，统一推广了Lewis（Green函子）和Nakaoka（Tambara函子）的素理想概念，并提供了多种计算工具及其应用实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lewis（1980年代）引入了Green函子中的素理想，Nakaoka（2010年代）引入了Tambara函子中的素理想，但缺乏一个统一框架来定义任意双不完全Tambara函子的素理想谱。

Method: 通过推广Lewis和Nakaoka的素理想定义，构建适用于一般双不完全Tambara函子的素理想谱理论，并开发配套的计算工具。

Result: 成功定义了双不完全Tambara函子的素理想谱，统一了已有的Green函子与Tambara函子的素理想概念，并给出了若干计算工具和具体例子的应用结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为双不完全Tambara函子的素理想谱建立了统一的理论框架，其计算工具可服务于后续的环论与等变同调代数研究。

Abstract: Bi-incomplete Tambara functors are equivariant generalizations of commutative rings. The most common forms of bi-incomplete Tambara functors are coefficient systems of commutative rings, Green functors, and Tambara functors. In the 1980s, Lewis introduced prime ideals in Green functors, and in the 2010s, Nakaoka introduced prime ideals in Tambara functors. In this work, we define the spectrum of prime ideals for an arbitrary bi-incomplete Tambara functor, simultaneously generalizing Lewis and Nakaoka's notions. We then produce many computational tools which we apply to several examples of interest.

</details>

### [102] [Error estimation for numerical approximations of ODEs via composition techniques. Part II: BDF methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06735)
*Ahmad Deeb,Denys Dutykh,Maryam Al Zohbi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将复系数组合技术应用于隐式BDF格式的方法，在不增加后向步数的情况下将近似阶数提升一阶，并打破了Dahlquist稳定性障碍，实现了高达八阶的稳定性。组合流的虚部可提供p+1阶的误差估计。在非均匀网格下，连续时间步长之比影响稳定性，并给出了不同阶数下的下界（如三阶0.4506，四阶0.6806）。数值实验验证了该方法在精度和稳定性上优于传统BDF方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BDF方法在阶数增加时面临Dahlquist稳定性障碍（高于二阶的BDF方法不再A-稳定），且高阶精度需要更多后向步数，计算成本高。作者希望在不增加后向步数的前提下提升精度，同时突破稳定性限制。

Method: 将复系数组合技术应用于隐式BDF格式，通过组合流的虚部构建误差估计，并分析线性稳定性条件；针对非均匀网格，推导时间步长比的下界以满足稳定性要求。

Result: 组合格式打破了Dahlquist稳定性障碍，实现了高达八阶的稳定性；计算性能优于同后向步数的BDF方案；非均匀网格下给出了三阶（0.4506）和四阶（0.6806）的步长比下界。

Conclusion: 复系数组合技术能有效提升隐式BDF方法的精度和稳定性，在相同后向步数下实现更高阶精度和更低计算时间，是一种实用的改进方案。

Abstract: Integration of Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) using Backward Difference formula (BDF) methods with p backward steps achieves order p accuracy if specific conditions are met. This work extends the composition technique with complex coefficients to the implicit BDF schemes, increasing the approximation order by one without additional backward points. The imaginary part of the composed flow provides an error estimate of order p + 1. Linear stability analysis reveals that the composed schemes break the Dahlquist barrier, achieving stability up to order eight. The computational performance of the composed flow outperforms BDF schemes when using the same number of backward points, allowing for higher accuracy with lower CPU time. For non-uniform meshes, the ratio of consecutive time steps, which influences stability, appears as a parameter in the roots of algebraic equations relative to the composed flow. Having a complex root with a real positive part implies a lower bound to this ratio depending on the order. For example, the bound is 0.4506 for order three and 0.6806 for order four. Numerical tests demonstrate the effectiveness of this technique in improving the accuracy and stability compared to BDF methods.

</details>


### [103] [Efficient symplectic integrators for cubic and quartic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06975)
*Alejandro Escorihuela-Tomàs*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有三次或四次势能的哈密顿系统，提出了一组新型高效高阶辛方法。通过利用多项式势能所需阶条件更少的特点，所开发的方法在效率上优于标准的对称组合二阶方法以及现有的RKN分裂方法。数值结果验证了其相较于文献中现有最先进方法的改进效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高阶辛方法在处理具有多项式势能的哈密顿系统时效率有限，标准对称组合和RKN分裂方法在阶条件数量上未充分利用多项式势能的特殊结构，导致计算成本较高。本文旨在利用多项式势能阶条件更少的特性，设计更高效的高阶辛方法。

Method: 论文分析了多项式势能（三次或四次）下哈密顿系统辛积分器的阶条件，发现此类系统所需满足的阶条件数量少于一般情况。基于这一理论发现，通过减少不必要的阶条件约束，构建了新的高阶辛格式，这些格式在保持辛结构的同时实现了更高的计算效率。

Result: 数值实验表明，新提出的高阶辛方法在效率和精度上均优于标准对称组合二阶方法以及现有的RKN分裂方法，特别是在处理三次/四次势能哈密顿系统时表现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 利用多项式势能的特殊结构可以有效减少高阶辛方法的阶条件数量，从而设计出更高效的数值积分方案。该方法为特定类型哈密顿系统的高效长时间模拟提供了新的途径。

Abstract: We present a set of new, efficient high-order symplectic methods designed for Hamiltonian systems with cubic or quartic potentials. By demonstrating that polynomial potentials require fewer order conditions, we develop schemes that outperform both standard symmetric compositions of second-order methods and existing RKN splitting methods. Numerical results confirm their improved efficiency over state-of-the-art alternatives found in the literature.

</details>


### [104] [Proximal Galerkin for the isometry constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07054)
*Brendan Keith,Frédéric Marazzato*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性板计算建模中长期存在的开放问题，提出了一种基于近端伽辽金框架的数值方法，能够精确强制等距约束（即中面第一基本形式与单位张量一致），无需预处理步骤，且迭代次数与网格无关，相比现有方法大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非线性板计算建模中精确强制等距约束这一长期开放问题，克服现有梯度流方法需要预处理步骤以及每一步梯度流都会增加等距缺陷的两大挑战。

Method: 采用基于近端伽辽金框架的替代方法，该方法最初是为凸不等式约束变分问题设计的。该方法保留了可行集的几何结构，使得每个迭代步在网格单元的每个重心处都是精确的等距。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在达到预设误差容限时收敛迭代次数渐近地与网格无关，并且即使在使用粗网格时，迭代次数也远少于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法无需预处理步骤，能够更广泛地适用于这类重要的数学模型，为非线性板计算建模提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We resolve a longstanding open problem in the computational modeling of nonlinear plates by introducing a numerical method that exactly enforces the isometry constraint, namely, that the first fundamental form of the mid-surface coincides with the identity tensor. Several numerical methods have been proposed to approximate solutions of such manifold-constrained variational problems using gradient flows with tangent space updates. However, this class of methods presents two main challenges. First, a preprocessing step is required to enforce the boundary conditions and generate an initial guess sufficiently close to an isometry. Second, each step of the gradient flow typically increases the isometry defect. We adopt an alternative approach based on the proximal Galerkin framework, originally introduced for variational problems with convex inequality constraints. The resulting method preserves the geometric structure of the feasible set and yields an efficient algorithm in which each iterate is an exact isometry at the barycenter of every mesh cell. In contrast to existing methods, no preprocessing step is required, enabling broader applicability of this important category of mathematical models. Numerical experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that the method converges to a prescribed error tolerance in an asymptotically mesh-independent number of iterations and requires substantially fewer iterations than previous methods, even on coarse meshes.

</details>


### [105] [On a stochastic column-block bregman method for nonlinear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07136)
*Wendi Bao,Naiyu Jiang,Lili Xing,Weiguo Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机列块非线性Bregman方法，用于高效计算非线性系统的稀疏解，并在一定假设下分析了收敛性及收敛速率上界，通过数值实验（包括图像恢复问题）验证了其效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏解问题在信号处理和图像恢复中具有重要作用，现有方法在处理大规模非线性系统时效率不足，因此需要提出更高效的求解方法。

Method: 提出了一种随机列块非线性Bregman方法，该方法结合了随机列块采样策略与非线性Bregman迭代框架。

Result: 在特定假设下证明了所提方法的收敛性，并推导出收敛速率的上界；数值实验（包括图像恢复问题）表明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机列块非线性Bregman方法能够高效求解非线性系统的稀疏解，在信号处理和图像恢复领域具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Sparse solution problems play an important role in both signal processing and image restoration. In this paper, we propose a stochastic column-block nonlinear Bregman method for efficiently computing sparse solutions to nonlinear systems. Under certain assumptions, we analyze the convergence of the proposed method and derive an upper bound for its convergence rate. Numerical experiments, including an image recovery problem, are presented to illustrate the efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [106] [Variable decoupling and the Kolmogorov Superposition Theorem for rational functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07246)
*A. C. Antoulas,I. V. Gosea,C. Poussot-Vassal*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文表明，对于有理多元函数，Kolmogorov叠加定理（KST）涉及的多个单变量函数可以通过观察直接写出，无需计算即可解耦多元有理函数的变量。关键工具是多变量Loewner框架。该结果可用于通过低复杂度的多元有理函数和多项式函数逼近非有理多元函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化多元有理函数的变量解耦过程，消除传统方法中所需的计算开销，从而更高效地实现函数分解与逼近。

Method: 利用多变量Loewner框架作为核心工具，将Kolmogorov叠加定理应用于有理多元函数，实现单变量函数的直接观察获取。

Result: 有理多元函数的KST分解中的单变量函数可通过观察直接写出，无需任何计算。

Conclusion: 该方法可应用于将非有理多元函数通过低复杂度的多元有理函数和多项式函数进行逼近。

Abstract: This work shows that for rational multivariate functions, the Kolmogorov Superposition Theorem (KST) involves several single-variable functions, which can be written down by inspection. In other words, no computation is required for decoupling the variables of multivariate rational functions. The key tool for this development is the Loewner Framework for multivariate functions. Applications of this result involve approximating multivariate non-rational functions by low-complexity multivariate rational and polynomial functions.

</details>


### [107] [Convergence of an Eulerian scheme for the Vlasov-Poisson-BGK model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07347)
*Seung Yeon Cho,Sungsu Park,Seok-Bae Yun*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次对全相空间网格上非分裂有限差分离散VPBGK模型进行了收敛性分析，通过提出修正下界估计克服了电场引起的速度索引混合这一稳定性挑战，并在截断速度域和Neumann边界条件下建立了分布函数和电场场的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有多种数值格式用于VPBGK模型，但缺乏相应的收敛性理论。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供首个收敛性分析。

Method: 针对非分裂有限差分离散格式在全相空间网格上，提出了修正下界估计来处理电场导致的速度索引混合问题，在截断速度域及Neumann边界条件下进行分析。

Result: 建立了分布函数在加权L∞范数下的误差估计以及电场在L∞范数下的误差估计。

Conclusion: 本文首次为VPBGK模型的非分裂有限差分格式提供了收敛性理论，提出的修正下界估计有效克服了离子化系统中的稳定性难题。

Abstract: The Vlasov-Poisson-BGK (VPBGK) model is a kinetic model for describing the dynamics of collisional plasmas. Although various numerical schemes have been developed for it, a corresponding convergence theory has been absent. This paper fills this gap by presenting the first convergence analysis for a non-splitting, finite-difference Eulerian scheme discretized on the full phase-space grid. A major theoretical obstacle is the mixing of velocity indices induced by the electric field, which hinders the derivation of a uniform lower bound for the discrete solution. To overcome this stability challenge, we propose a modified lower bound estimate suitable for ionized systems that incorporates the step-wise degradation. Under a truncated velocity domain with a Neumann boundary condition, we establish error estimates for the distribution function in a weighted $L^{\infty}$ norm and for the electric field in a $L^{\infty}$ norm, respectively.

</details>


### [108] [Solving Convolution-type Integral Equations using Preconditioned Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07365)
*Raymond Chan,Lingfeng Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将神经算子与经典迭代求解器（如加权Jacobi方法）相结合的混合迭代算法，用于高效求解卷积型积分方程。通过预条件训练策略让神经算子专门处理高频误差分量，从而显著优于多重网格法和预条件共轭梯度法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积型积分方程在信号处理和图像去模糊等领域广泛应用，但传统数值方法（如多重网格法）仅能有效求解误差中的低频分量，而无法高效处理高频分量，亟需一种能够同时处理高低频分量的高效求解方法。

Method: 采用预条件方法，设计了一种新的训练策略，训练神经算子高效求解高频误差分量；然后将神经算子与经典迭代求解器（如加权Jacobi方法）结合，形成混合迭代算法。

Result: 在从一维和二维卷积型积分方程离散得到的大规模、病态线性系统上进行了测试，所提算法在迭代次数和计算时间上均显著优于多重网格法和预条件共轭梯度法。

Conclusion: 所提出的神经算子与经典迭代求解器结合的混合算法能有效解决传统方法对高频分量处理效率低的问题，在卷积型积分方程求解中展现出优越的性能。

Abstract: Convolution-type integral equations arise from various fields, \textit{e.g.}, finite impulse response filters in signal processing and deblurring problems in image processing. When solving these equations, conventional numerical methods, like the multigrid method, can only efficiently solve the low-frequency components in the error, but not the high-frequency components. In this paper, we apply neural operators to address this issue. By adopting a preconditioning approach, we propose a novel training strategy that trains neural operators to solve the high-frequency components efficiently. Then, we combine the neural operators with some classical iterative solvers, like the weighted Jacobi method, to obtain an efficient hybrid iterative algorithm for the integral equations. We analyze the generalization error of our training strategy and the convergence of the hybrid iterative algorithm. We test our algorithms on large-scale and ill-conditioned linear systems discretized from one- and two-dimensional convolution-type integral equations. Our proposed algorithm significantly outperforms the multigrid method and the preconditioned conjugate gradient method in both iteration numbers and computational time.

</details>


### [109] [Kolmogorov $\varepsilon$-entropy of numerical solutions for scalar conservation laws with convex flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07427)
*Fabio Ancona,Alessio Basti,Fabio Camilli*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有一致凸通量的标量守恒律，建立了数值解在Kolmogorov ε-熵意义下的双向定量紧致性估计。研究表明，满足离散单侧Lipschitz条件的守恒单调有限差分格式能够保持精确熵解集的1/ε熵标度，从而证明了一阶方法在Lax意义下的高分辨率特性，并提出了通用的下界传递原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Lax信息论视角下的守恒律解紧致性理论推广到数值解领域，量化数值方法在熵解集逼近中的信息论分辨率，建立精确解与数值解之间Kolmogorov熵标度的一致性。

Method: 基于信息论框架，利用Kolmogorov ε-熵作为定量工具，通过离散单侧Lipschitz条件证明上界，借助有界变差前驱类的一致逼近论证建立下界，并将下界机制抽象为通用传递原理。

Result: 证明了保守单调有限差分格式在特定网格约束下保持1/ε的Kolmogorov熵标度，匹配了De Lellis-Golse和Ancona-Glass-Nguyen的理论结果；揭示了一阶格式在Lax信息分辨率意义下的高分辨率特性。

Conclusion: 数值格式能够保持熵解集的信息论复杂度，一阶方法具有Lax意义下的高分辨率；下界传递原理可用于信息恢复和后处理，为未来高分辨率方法设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: Building on the information-theoretic perspective of P.~D.~Lax [\textit{Proc.\ Sympos., Math.\ Res.\ Center, Univ.\ Wisconsin}, 1978], we establish a two-sided quantitative compactness estimate for numerical solutions of scalar conservation laws with a uniformly convex flux, expressed in terms of Kolmogorov $\varepsilon$-entropy. We prove that, under specific grid constraints, conservative, monotone finite-difference schemes satisfying a discrete one-sided Lipschitz condition (OSLC) preserve the $1/\varepsilon$ Kolmogorov entropy scaling of the corresponding exact entropy solution set, matching the bounds obtained by De~Lellis and Golse [\textit{Comm.\ Pure Appl.\ Math.}\ \textbf{58} (2005)] and by Ancona, Glass, and Nguyen [\textit{Comm.\ Pure Appl.\ Math.}\ \textbf{65} (2012)]. Specifically, the upper bound follows from the discrete OSLC, while the lower bound relies on a uniform approximation argument on a bounded-variation precursor class. Our results show that prototypical first-order methods are high-resolution in Lax's sense. Finally, we abstract the lower bound mechanism into a general transfer principle, discuss implications for information recovery via post-processing, and indicate directions for future work.

</details>


### [110] [On structure-preserving and pointwise conservative continuous DG schemes for hyperbolic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07576)
*Rémi Abgrall,Michael Dumbser,Pierre-Henri Maire,Enrico Zampa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的保结构CG-DG有限元格式，用于求解非结构单纯形网格上的线性和非线性双曲型偏微分方程组。该格式同时满足：逐点局部守恒性、矢量微积分恒等式的精确离散满足，以及线性对称双曲系统的能量守恒性（L²范数非线性稳定性）。关键思想是使用两种不同但相容的逼近空间：N次间断分片多项式DG空间U_h^N和N+1次全局连续分片多项式有限元空间W_h^{N+1}。对于N=0，该格式直接关联到基于顶点的单元中心有限体积格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值格式难以同时满足逐点局部守恒性、矢量恒等式的精确离散保持以及能量稳定性。本文旨在设计一种能够自动满足这些保结构性质的新型数值格式，以更精确地模拟双曲型守恒律系统。

Method: 采用两种兼容的逼近空间：DG空间U_h^N（间断多项式）用于离散解u_h，连续有限元空间W_h^{N+1}（连续多项式）用于计算通量场f̃_h。通过这种CG-DG耦合策略，在非结构单纯形网格上构造半离散格式，实现逐点守恒性、矢量恒等式精确成立和能量守恒性。

Result: 数学证明了格式的三个核心性质：逐点局部守恒性（任意控制体积上成立）、矢量恒等式∇·∇×A和∇×∇Z在离散层面精确满足、线性对称双曲系统的L²能量守恒。数值算例验证了理论分析的正确性，展示了在线性和非线性双曲系统中的有效应用。

Conclusion: 提出的CG-DG格式是一类兼具DG和CG优势的保结构数值方法，为双曲型守恒律的高保真模拟提供了新工具。N=0时与经典顶点基单元中心有限体积格式的自然联系，显示了其广泛的适用性和理论价值。

Abstract: We present a new class of structure-preserving semi-discrete continuous-discontinuous Galerkin (CG-DG) finite element schemes for linear and nonlinear hyperbolic systems of partial differential equations on unstructured simplex meshes that automatically satisfy the following properties: i) the new schemes are not only cellwise conservative, but also locally pointwise conservative everywhere, hence they satisfy the integral form of the conservation law on arbitrary control volumes that do not have to coincide with the mesh at all; ii) the new methods naturally satisfy the two basic vector calculus identities $\nabla \cdot \nabla \times \mathbf{A}$ and $\nabla \times \nabla Z$ exactly pointwise locally and globally everywhere on the discrete level; iii) for linear symmetric hyperbolic systems the schemes are naturally energy conservative for the square energy, i.e. nonlinearly stable in the $L^2$ norm. The key ingredient of the new CG-DG schemes is the use of two different but compatible approximation spaces: the classical DG space $\mathcal{U}_h^N$ of discontinuous piecewise polynomials of degree up to $N$ and a classical finite element space $\mathcal{W}_h^{N+1}$ of globally continuous piecewise polynomials of degree $N+1$. In the new CG-DG schemes, the discrete solution $\mathbf{u}_h$ is sought in $\mathcal{U}_h^N$, while a suitable discrete flux field $\tilde{\mathbf{f}}_h$ is computed in $\mathcal{W}_h^{N+1}$. For $N=0$ our new schemes are directly related to cell-centered finite volume schemes with suitable vertex-based fluxes. All claimed properties of the schemes are first mathematically proven and are then also verified via suitable numerical tests. We show applications of our approach to three linear and nonlinear hyperbolic systems.

</details>


### [111] [Invariant domain preserving limiting of time explicit and time implicit discretizations for systems of conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08038)
*Bartolomeo Fanizza,Florent Renac*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种保持非线性双曲守恒律系统高阶数值近似中不变域的限制技术，该技术可应用于任意保守空间离散方法和多种时间积分格式，通过限制反扩散通量并以凸组合形式表达限制解，推广了通量校正传输限制器至方程组系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种能够保持高阶数值格式近似非线性双曲守恒律系统时不变域的限制技术，将经典的通量校正传输（FCT）限制器从标量方程推广到方程组系统。

Method: 基于凸限制框架，将高阶解围绕已知保持不变域的低阶解进行限制；通过限制反扩散通量定义限制系数，使限制解表达为保持不变域量的凸组合；采用迭代方式逐步逼近高阶解，并提出了加速收敛的启发式策略。

Result: 该技术适用于有限体积和间断伽辽金（DG）空间离散，以及显式/隐式龙格-库塔和时间DG积分格式；通过一维和二维标量双曲方程及可压缩欧拉方程的数值实验验证了格式的特性。

Conclusion: 所提出的限制技术能有效保持不变域，同时可推广至多种离散方法和时间积分格式，迭代策略有助于在保持不变域的前提下逼近高阶解精度。

Abstract: This work concerns the design and analysis of a limiting technique that allows the preservation of invariant domains for high-order numerical approximations of nonlinear hyperbolic systems of conservation laws. The method can be applied to any conservative discretization method in space as well as to a wide range of explicit and implicit time integration schemes. The method limits the high-order solution around a low-order accurate solution that is known to preserve all the invariant domains. It generalizes the flux-corrected transport limiter [J. P. Boris and D. L. Book, J. Comput. Phys., 11, 1973; S. T. Zalesak, J. Comput. Phys., 31, 1979] to systems of conservation laws and relies on the limitation of antidiffusive fluxes, but defines the limiting coefficients so as to express the limited solution as a convex combination of invariant domain preserving quantities similarly to the convex limiting framework [Guermond et al., Comput. Methods Appl. Mech. Engrg., 347, 2019]. We give details on the derivation of this limiting technique and provide some illustration with finite volume or discontinuous Galerkin (DG) space discretizations associated to explicit or implicit Runge-Kutta methods as well as to time DG integrations. The limiter is applied iteratively to refine the limited solution around the high-order one, while preserving the invariant domains, and a heuristic is proposed to accelerate its convergence. Numerical experiments solving one- and two-dimensional problems involving scalar hyperbolic equations and the compressible Euler equations are presented to illustrate the properties of these schemes.

</details>
